You are adding a "ten-second reliability read" component to the Faultline web app at /Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web (Vite + React 19 + TS 6 + Tailwind v4 + shadcn base-nova on Base UI, dark theme). Repo root /Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic.

The app shows runs of a Claude agent working in a fault-injecting sandbox. A reviewer must answer, in ten seconds, without reading the transcript: what was the task, what failed and where (simulated vs real, which layer), did the command execute (failed execution vs missing acknowledgement vs unknown are DIFFERENT answers), how did recovery work (agent actions vs harness retries, did the sandbox survive), and did the result pass independent checks. Technical codes must be explained in plain language with the raw details expandable.

Component: src/components/verdict/VerdictStrip.tsx (+ index.ts, + VerdictStrip.test.tsx)

  • Layout: a card at the top of the transcript, five panels in a row on ≥1024 px (grid), stacked below. Each panel: a status glyph (ok ✓ emerald, warn ⚠ amber, fail ✕ rose, unknown ? muted, pending ◌ animated), a small uppercase label (TASK / FAULT / EXECUTION / RECOVERY / RESULT), the headline in medium weight, the plain sentence in text-ink-3, then facts as small chips (label: value; chip tone by fact.tone; a tooltip with fact.hint when present). Keep the whole strip readable in ten seconds: no more than ~3 lines per panel by default.
  • "Details" toggle per panel (Collapsible, aria-expanded) reveals: (fault) the incident list with origin badge (verbatim taxonomy label), layer, code + plain-language dictionary text, path, step, planned marker; (execution) per incident the outcome word with its source ("from error class" / "from the ledger after grading" / "exit code" / "unclassified") and side_effect_applied when known; (recovery) the follow-up calls (read-back / re-write with ok/failed), harness attempts, worker generation/resumes, sandbox survived/lost; (result) diff paths with +/−, tests, every check with description and ✓/✕ and detail; (task) the full task prompt. Under each details section a <details> "raw" block showing raw[].json via CodeBlock (language json) or a monospace <pre>.
  • A compact SandboxChip (export it) for the top bar: episode id short + "worker n" + survived/lost/unknown, tone accordingly.
  • Accessibility: the strip is a <section aria-label="Run verdict">; panels are <article>s; glyphs have role="img" + aria-label.
  • Tests (vitest + Testing Library): render fixtures for (1) an injected ack_lost run that recovered and scored 100 — headlines visible, origin badge says "simulated", execution shows "executed" with ledger source; (2) a real sandbox loss (fail, "real" badge, sandbox lost, result blocked); (3) all-unknown (pre-taxonomy record) shows "unknown" wording, never a fabricated outcome; (4) details toggle reveals the raw JSON.

Report: the exported components and props, the fixture shapes you used, and final tsc/vitest/oxlint summaries.

/**
 * The ten-second reliability read of a run, as data.
 *
 * Contract (ARCHITECTURE.md §5.4, docs/error-taxonomy.md): every field here comes from structured
 * event fields — `error_class`, `fault.fired`, `interruption`, `run.resumed`, the evaluate ledger,
 * `episode.evaluated`, `workspace.diff` — never from parsing an error message. Anything the record
 * does not carry is `unknown`, and the raw payloads behind every panel are kept for an expander.
 */

import type { ErrorClass, ErrorLayer, ErrorOrigin, Interruption, RunStatus } from './types'

export type PanelStatus = 'ok' | 'warn' | 'fail' | 'unknown' | 'pending'

export type PanelKey = 'task' | 'fault' | 'execution' | 'recovery' | 'result'

/** A raw payload shown under "details": the event(s) or record fields a panel was built from. */
export interface RawRef {
  label: string
  json: unknown
}

export interface Fact {
  label: string
  value: string
  tone?: PanelStatus
  /** Plain-language explanation shown in a tooltip / expander (e.g. what a code means). */
  hint?: string
}

export interface VerdictPanel {
  key: PanelKey
  status: PanelStatus
  /** The one line a reviewer reads in ten seconds (≤ ~70 chars). */
  headline: string
  /** One or two plain-language sentences. */
  plain: string
  facts: Fact[]
  raw: RawRef[]
}

/** How a failed/faulted call actually ended, and where that knowledge comes from. */
export type ExecutionOutcome = 'executed' | 'failed' | 'not_executed' | 'unknown'
export type ExecutionSource = 'error_class' | 'ledger' | 'exit_code' | 'none'

export interface FollowUp {
  step: number
  tool: string
  toolUseId: string
  kind: 'read' | 'write' | 'other'
  ok: boolean | null
}

/** One failure the run hit: an injected/staged fault, a real error on a call, or an interruption. */
export interface Incident {
  id: string
  step: number | null
  tool: string | null
  toolUseId: string | null
  path: string | null
  origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown'
  layer: ErrorLayer | null
  code: string | null
  /** ErrorClass.label verbatim when present, else the fixed label for (origin, kind) from the taxonomy, else null. */
  label: string | null
  kind: string | null
  /** interruption.planned — a deliberate real failure (harness_faults). null when not an interruption. */
  planned: boolean | null
  execution: ExecutionOutcome
  executionSource: ExecutionSource
  /** error_class.side_effect_applied, or the ledger's answer once evaluate released it. */
  sideEffectApplied: boolean | null
  outcomeKnown: boolean | null
  /** Later calls touching the same path, in order — the agent's recovery actions. */
  followUps: FollowUp[]
  errorClass: ErrorClass | null
  interruption: Interruption | null
  raw: RawRef[]
}

export interface GradingCheck {
  id: string
  ok: boolean
  weight: number
  /** Plain language for the check id (src/lib/checks.ts), falling back to the id. */
  description: string
  detail: string
}

export interface Grading {
  /** true once episode.evaluated arrived */
  available: boolean
  score: number | null
  tests: { passed: number; failed: number; errors: number } | null
  checks: GradingCheck[]
  /** Why grading did not happen (status unevaluated / interrupted / error): the ErrorClass label. */
  blockedBy: string | null
}

export interface SandboxSummary {
  episodeId: string | null
  workerGeneration: number
  resumes: number
  /** true = an interruption/error_class with layer "sandbox" was observed; false = the run was graded inside it; null = cannot say. */
  lost: boolean | null
  lostAtStep: number | null
}

export interface DiffSummary {
  files: number
  added: number
  removed: number
  paths: string[]
}

export interface Verdict {
  status: RunStatus | null
  live: boolean
  panels: Record<PanelKey, VerdictPanel>
  incidents: Incident[]
  sandbox: SandboxSummary
  grading: Grading
  diff: DiffSummary | null
}

/**
 * Wire contracts mirrored from packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py.
 *
 * Keep field names identical to the pydantic models — the harness serialises those models
 * straight onto the wire. If a field is missing here it is simply not rendered; if a field is
 * added here it MUST be optional so an older harness still type-checks at runtime.
 */

export type FaultMode = 'sticky' | 'transient'

  mode: FaultMode
  hits: number | null
  delay_ms: number
}

Tool reply2026-09-12T22:33:10.694Z
# Error and status taxonomy (contract for the UI, the ledger and the evidence)

Two questions must be answerable for every failure a run shows: **who caused it** (`origin`) and
**which layer really failed** (`layer`). The agent never gets these; the UI always does.

## `ErrorClass` (on every `tool.result` with `is_error`, on `fault.fired`, on `RunRecord.error_class`)

```json
{"origin": "injected|staged|real", "layer": "boundary|filesystem|sandbox|transport|harness|model|gym",
 "code": "ENOENT|EACCES|ETIMEDOUT|EINVAL|ESANDBOX|ETRANSPORT|EHARNESS|EMODEL|EGYM|EINTERNAL|ENOEPISODE",
 "kind": "missing_file|denied_write|ack_lost|null", "label": "<fixed string below>",
 "outcome_known": true|false, "side_effect_applied": true|false|null, "detail": "…"}
```

| origin / layer | code(s) | label (render verbatim) | outcome_known | side effect |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| injected / boundary | ENOENT | `simulated: missing file (file still on disk)` | true | no |
| injected / boundary | EACCES | `simulated: write denied (nothing written)` | true | no |
| injected / boundary | ETIMEDOUT | `simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)` | **false** | **yes** |
| staged / filesystem | ENOENT | `staged: file absent since reset` | true | n/a |
| real / filesystem | ENOENT, EACCES, EINVAL | `real: OS error in sandbox` | true | no |
| real / sandbox | ESANDBOX | `real: sandbox terminated or unavailable` | true | no |
| real / transport | ETRANSPORT, ETIMEDOUT | `real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)` | **false** | unknown |
| real / harness | EHARNESS | `real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)` | **false** | unknown (ledger has truth after the fact) |
| real / model | EMODEL | `real: model API error` | true | — |
| real / gym | EGYM | `real: gym control-plane failure` | true | — |
| real / boundary | EINTERNAL, ENOEPISODE | `real: internal error in sandbox-env` | true | no |

UI guidance: badge colour by `origin` (injected = amber "simulated", staged = amber "staged", real
= red "real"); show `label`; when `outcome_known: false` add "verify before retrying"; when the
ledger later resolves `side_effect_applied`, show it ("the write had landed").

## Events

- `fault.fired` — `FaultFired` + `origin` (`injected` or `staged`). Never `real`.
- `interruption` — `Interruption` (always real): `{step, layer, code, label, tool_use_id?, tool?, path?, outcome_known, planned, resumed, worker_generation, at, detail}`. `planned: true` means a scenario `harness_faults` entry triggered it (a deliberate real failure).
- `run.resumed` — `{worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}` emitted by the worker that picked the run up.
- `tool.result` — `data.error_class` present iff `is_error`; `data.fault` (FaultFired) present iff a fault fired for this call.

## Run status

| status | meaning | score |
|---|---|---|
| `ok` | the agent submitted or ended; evaluation succeeded | number |
| `truncated` | step budget exhausted; still evaluated | number |
| `unevaluated` | the loop finished but `evaluate` failed (`error_class.layer: gym` or `sandbox`) — **not** the agent's fault | null |
| `interrupted` | a real interruption ended the run and no worker resumed it | null |
| `error` | the harness could not run the episode (model auth, reset failed, bug) | null |

`RunRecord.interruptions[]` lists every real interruption, `worker_generation` is 1 + the number of
resumes; a resumed run can still end `ok` (that is the point of the `worker-crash` scenario).

## Scenario metadata

`Scenario.fault_kinds` (injected kinds in play) and `Scenario.harness_faults[]` (planned **real**
interruptions: `{kind: worker_crash|transport_abort, tool, path, nth, after_ms}`) are public so the
UI can show "includes a real harness interruption" on the card. The agent is never told either.

## Ledger (released at evaluate)

`LedgerEntry.origin` (`injected|staged|real`, only when `outcome != ok`), `error_code`, and
`interrupted: true` when the harness reported that it never received the response (`POST
/episodes/{id}/interruptions`). `verified_before_rewrite` treats `ack_lost` rows and `interrupted`
rows identically.

  gauntlet: Zap,

const STATUS_DOT: Record<RunStatus, string> = {
  queued: 'bg-muted-foreground/60',
  running: 'bg-sky-400 animate-pulse',
  ok: 'bg-emerald-400',
  error: 'bg-rose-400',
  truncated: 'bg-amber-400',
}

function dayBucket(iso: string, now = new Date()): string {
  const d = new Date(iso)
  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return 'Earlier'
  const startOfToday = new Date(now.getFullYear(), now.getMonth(), now.getDate()).getTime()
  const t = d.getTime()
  if (t >= startOfToday) return 'Today'
  if (t >= startOfToday - 86_400_000) return 'Yesterday'
  if (t >= startOfToday - 6 * 86_400_000) return 'Previous 7 days'
  return 'Earlier'
}

function groupByDay(items: ConversationSummary[]): { label: string; items: ConversationSummary[] }[] {
  const order = ['Today', 'Yesterday', 'Previous 7 days', 'Earlier']
  const buckets = new Map<string, ConversationSummary[]>()
  for (const c of items) {
    const key = dayBucket(c.updated_at || c.created_at)
    const arr = buckets.get(key) ?? []
    arr.push(c)
    buckets.set(key, arr)
  }
  return order.filter((k) => buckets.has(k)).map((k) => ({ label: k, items: buckets.get(k)! }))
}

function shortId(userId: string | null): string {
  if (!userId) return '—'
  return userId.length > 12 ? `${userId.slice(0, 10)}…` : userId
}

function ConversationRow({ c, active }: { c: ConversationSummary; active: boolean }) {
  const Icon = SCENARIO_ICON[c.scenario_id] ?? MessageSquare
  const status = c.last_run?.status ?? null
  const route: Route = { kind: 'conversation', id: c.id }
  return (
    <SidebarMenuItem>
      <SidebarMenuButton
        isActive={active}
        tooltip={c.title}
        render={<a {...linkProps(route)} />}
        className="group/row"
      >
        <Icon aria-hidden className="text-muted-foreground" />
        <span className="truncate">{c.title}</span>
      </SidebarMenuButton>
      {status ? (
        <SidebarMenuBadge className="gap-1.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-muted-foreground">
          {c.last_run?.score !== null && c.last_run?.score !== undefined
            ? Math.round(c.last_run.score)
            : ''}
          <span
            aria-label={`last run ${status}`}
            className={`inline-block size-1.5 rounded-full ${STATUS_DOT[status]}`}
          />
        </SidebarMenuBadge>
      ) : null}

31	  return (
32	    <div className="hidden flex-col leading-tight lg:flex" title={title}>
33	      <span className="text-[10px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">{label}</span>

39	export function RunStatusStrip({
40	  state,
41	  transport,
42	  source,
43	  replaying,
44	}: {
45	  state: ViewState | null
46	  transport: TransportState | null
47	  source: TranscriptSource | null
48	  replaying?: boolean
49	}) {
50	  const live = !!state && (state.status === 'running' || state.status === 'queued')
51	  const [, setTick] = useState(0)
52	  useEffect(() => {
53	    if (!live) return
54	    const t = setInterval(() => setTick((n) => n + 1), 1000)
55	    return () => clearInterval(t)
56	  }, [live])
57	
58	  if (!state) return null
59	  const faults = faultCount(state)
60	  const recovered = recoveredCount(state)
61	  const src = source ? SOURCE_LABEL[source] : null
62	  const SrcIcon = src?.icon ?? Radio
63	
64	  return (
65	    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
66	      <Badge variant="outline" className={`font-mono ${STATUS_TONE[state.status]}`}>
67	        {replaying ? 'replay' : state.status}
68	      </Badge>
69	      {src ? (
70	        <Tooltip>
71	          <TooltipTrigger
72	            render={
73	              <Badge variant="outline" className="font-mono text-muted-foreground">
74	                <SrcIcon aria-hidden /> {src.label}
75	              </Badge>
76	            }
77	          />
78	          <TooltipContent>{src.tip}{transport?.detail ? ` (${transport.detail})` : ''}</TooltipContent>
79	        </Tooltip>
80	      ) : null}
81	      <Stat label="step" value={`${state.step}/${state.maxSteps}`} />
82	      <Stat
83	        label="tokens"
84	        value={`${fmtTokens(state.usage.input_tokens)} in · ${fmtTokens(state.usage.output_tokens)} out`}
85	        title={`${state.usage.input_tokens} input / ${state.usage.output_tokens} output`}
86	      />
87	      <Stat label="elapsed" value={fmtDuration(elapsedMs(state))} />
88	      <Stat label="faults" value={`${recovered}/${faults} recovered`} />
89	      {state.evaluation ? <Stat label="score" value={`${Math.round(state.evaluation.score)}`} /> : null}
90	    </div>
91	  )
92	}

89	export function TranscriptView({
90	  transcript,
91	  diffs,
92	  maxSteps,
93	  loading,
94	  loadingLabel,
95	  error,
96	  transport,
97	  footer,
98	}: {
99	  transcript: Transcript
100	  diffs: FileDiff[]
101	  maxSteps: number
102	  loading?: boolean
103	  loadingLabel?: string
104	  error?: string | null
105	  transport?: TransportState | null
106	  /** Rendered below the transcript inside the same scroll container (run switcher, composer spacer). */
107	  footer?: React.ReactNode
108	}) {
109	  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null)
110	  const stick = useRef(true)
111	  const live = transcript.live
112	
113	  useEffect(() => {
114	    const el = ref.current
115	    if (!el) return
116	    const onScroll = () => {
117	      stick.current = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < 96
118	    }
119	    el.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true })
120	    return () => el.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
121	  }, [])
122	
123	  const turnCount = transcript.turns.length
124	  const callCount = transcript.turns.reduce((n, t) => n + t.calls.length, 0)
125	  useEffect(() => {
126	    const el = ref.current
127	    if (!el || !live || !stick.current) return
128	    el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight
129	  }, [turnCount, callCount, live, transcript.evaluation])
130	
131	  const last = transcript.turns[transcript.turns.length - 1]
132	  const waitingOnModel =
133	    live && (!last || (last.calls.length > 0 && last.calls.every((c) => c.result !== null)))
134	
135	  return (
136	    <div ref={ref} className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-y-auto" tabIndex={0} aria-label="Transcript">
137	      <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-6 px-4 py-6 md:px-6">
138	        {loading ? (
139	          <LoadingState label={loadingLabel ?? 'Loading…'} />
140	        ) : null}
141	
142	        {error && turnCount === 0 && !loading ? (
143	          <Callout tone="warn" title="Could not load this run" body={error} />
144	        ) : null}
145	
146	        {transcript.taskPrompt ? <UserBubble text={transcript.taskPrompt} /> : null}
147	
148	        {transcript.turns.map((t, i) => (
149	          <TurnBlock
150	            key={t.step}
151	            turn={t}
152	            isLast={i === transcript.turns.length - 1}
153	            live={live}
154	            maxSteps={maxSteps}
155	            diffs={diffs}
156	          />
157	        ))}
158	
159	        {live && turnCount === 0 && !loading ? (
160	          <LoadingState
161	            label={transcript.status === 'queued' ? 'Queued — provisioning the sandbox…' : 'Provisioning the sandbox…'}
162	            sublabel="creating a Modal Sandbox, copying the fixture repo, applying the fault plan"
163	          />
164	        ) : null}
165	        {waitingOnModel && turnCount > 0 ? (
166	          <LoadingState label="Waiting on the model…" sublabel={transcript.model ?? undefined} />
167	        ) : null}
168	
169	        {transcript.evaluation ? <ScoreRows evaluation={transcript.evaluation} /> : null}
170	
171	        {transcript.status === 'error' ? (
172	          <Callout tone="error" title="The run ended with an error" body={transcript.error ?? null} />
173	        ) : transcript.error && !transcript.live ? (
174	          <Callout
175	            tone="warn"
176	            title={transcript.evaluation ? 'The run reported an error' : 'The run finished but could not be graded'}
177	            body={transcript.error}
178	          />
179	        ) : null}
180	        {transcript.status === 'truncated' ? (
181	          <Callout
182	            tone="warn"
183	            title={`Step budget exhausted (${maxSteps} steps)`}
184	            body="The agent did not submit in time; whatever it left on disk was still graded."
185	          />
186	        ) : null}
187	        {transport?.mode === 'poll' ? (
188	          <p className="text-center text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
189	            live stream unavailable — polling the run record every 1.5 s
190	          </p>
191	        ) : null}
192	        {footer}
193	      </div>
194	    </div>
195	  )

1	import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
2	import { AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'

4	import { Composer } from '@/components/bui'
5	import { linkProps } from '@/components/layout/Link'
6	import { RunStatusStrip, STATUS_TONE, type TranscriptSource } from '@/components/layout/RunStatusStrip'
7	import { TopBar } from '@/components/layout/TopBar'
8	import { TranscriptView } from '@/components/transcript/TranscriptView'
9	import { WorkspacePanel, useWorkspaceOpen } from '@/components/workspace/WorkspaceColumn'
10	import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
11	import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton'
12	import { useConversation } from '@/hooks/useConversation'
13	import type { ConversationsState } from '@/hooks/useConversations'
14	import type { HarnessState } from '@/hooks/useHarness'

22	const log = getLogger('web')

24	function RunSwitcher({
25	  conversationId,
26	  runs,
27	  selectedId,
28	}: {
29	  conversationId: string
30	  runs: RunSummary[]
31	  selectedId: string | null
32	}) {
33	  if (runs.length <= 1) return null
34	  return (
35	    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5" aria-label="Runs in this conversation">
36	      <span className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">runs</span>
37	      {runs.map((r, i) => (
38	        <a
39	          key={r.id}
40	          {...linkProps({ kind: 'conversation', id: conversationId, runId: r.id })}
41	          aria-current={r.id === selectedId ? 'page' : undefined}
42	          className="rounded-md outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50"
43	        >
44	          <Badge
45	            variant="outline"
46	            className={`font-mono ${STATUS_TONE[r.status]} ${r.id === selectedId ? 'ring-1 ring-ring/60' : 'opacity-80'}`}
47	          >
48	            #{i + 1} · {r.status}
49	            {r.score !== null && r.score !== undefined ? ` · ${Math.round(r.score)}` : ''}
50	          </Badge>
51	        </a>
52	      ))}
53	    </div>
54	  )
55	}

57	export function ConversationPage({
58	  harness,
59	  conversations,
60	  conversationId,
61	  runId,
62	}: {
63	  harness: HarnessState
64	  conversations: ConversationsState
65	  conversationId: string
66	  runId: string | null
67	}) {
68	  const { detail, loading, error, refresh } = useConversation(harness.client, conversationId)
69	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
70	  const [model, setModel] = useState<string>(DEFAULT_MODEL)
71	  const [seed, setSeed] = useState<number | null>(null)
72	  const [prompt, setPrompt] = useState('')
73	  const { start, busy, error: startError } = useStartRun(harness, conversations)
74	
75	  const runs = useMemo(
76	    () => [...(detail?.runs ?? [])].sort((a, b) => a.created_at.localeCompare(b.created_at)),
77	    [detail?.runs],
78	  )
79	  const selected: RunSummary | null = useMemo(() => {
80	    if (runs.length === 0) return null
81	    return (runId ? runs.find((r) => r.id === runId) : null) ?? runs[runs.length - 1] ?? null
82	  }, [runs, runId])
83	
84	  const source = useMemo(
85	    () => (selected ? ({ kind: 'live', runId: selected.id } as const) : ({ kind: 'none' } as const)),
86	    [selected],
87	  )
88	  const rv = useRunView(harness.client, source)
89	  const live = !!selected && (rv.state.status === 'running' || rv.state.status === 'queued')
90	
91	  // When the live run reaches a terminal state, reload the conversation from the store so the
92	  // transcript switches to the persisted (SQLite) projection and the rail updates its status dot.
93	  const refreshedFor = useRef<string | null>(null)
94	  useEffect(() => {
95	    if (!selected || !isTerminal(rv.state.status) || rv.state.runId !== selected.id) return
96	    if (refreshedFor.current === selected.id) return
97	    refreshedFor.current = selected.id
98	    if (!isTerminal(selected.status)) {
99	      log.info('conversation.refresh', 'run finished; reloading persisted transcript', {
100	        conversation_id: conversationId,
101	        run_id: selected.id,
102	        status: rv.state.status,
103	      })
104	      refresh()
105	      conversations.refresh()
106	    }
107	  }, [rv.state.status, rv.state.runId, selected, conversationId, refresh, conversations])
108	
109	  useEffect(() => {
110	    if (harness.health?.model_default) setModel(harness.health.model_default)
111	  }, [harness.health?.model_default])
112	
113	  const scenario = useMemo(
114	    () => harness.scenarios.find((s) => s.id === detail?.conversation.scenario_id) ?? null,
115	    [harness.scenarios, detail?.conversation.scenario_id],
116	  )
117	  useEffect(() => {
118	    if (scenario && prompt === '') setPrompt(scenario.task_prompt)
119	    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
120	  }, [scenario?.id])
121	
122	  useEffect(() => {
123	    document.title = detail ? `${detail.conversation.title} — Faultline` : 'Faultline'
124	  }, [detail])
125	
126	  const messagesForRun = useMemo(
127	    () => (detail && selected ? detail.messages.filter((m) => m.run_id === selected.id) : []),
128	    [detail, selected],
129	  )
130	  const persisted = !!detail && !!selected && isTerminal(selected.status) && messagesForRun.length > 0 && !live
131	  const transcript = useMemo(
132	    () =>
133	      persisted && detail && selected
134	        ? transcriptFromMessages(messagesForRun, selected)
135	        : transcriptFromViewState(rv.state, live),
136	    [persisted, detail, selected, messagesForRun, rv.state, live],
137	  )
138	  const transcriptSource: TranscriptSource | null = persisted
139	    ? 'sqlite'
140	    : live
141	      ? rv.transport?.mode === 'poll'
142	        ? 'poll'
143	        : 'sse'
144	      : selected
145	        ? 'record'
146	        : null
147	
148	  const submit = () => {
149	    if (!detail || busy) return
150	    void start({
151	      scenarioId: detail.conversation.scenario_id,
152	      model,
153	      seed,
154	      prompt: scenario && prompt.trim() === scenario.task_prompt.trim() ? null : prompt,
155	      conversationId: detail.conversation.id,
156	    })
157	  }
158	
159	  const title = detail?.conversation.title ?? (loading ? 'Loading…' : 'Conversation')
160	
161	  return (
162	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
163	      <TopBar
164	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Conversations', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: title }]}
165	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
166	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
167	      >
168	        <RunStatusStrip state={selected ? rv.state : null} transport={rv.transport} source={transcriptSource} />
169	      </TopBar>
170	
171	      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">
172	        <div className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
173	          {loading && !detail ? (
174	            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-4 px-4 py-8">
175	              <Skeleton className="ml-auto h-16 w-2/3" />
176	              <Skeleton className="h-20 w-full" />
177	              <Skeleton className="h-32 w-full" />
178	            </div>
179	          ) : error && !detail ? (
180	            <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl px-4 py-8">
181	              <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 px-3 py-2.5 text-[13px] text-amber-200">
182	                <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
183	                <div>
184	                  <div className="font-medium">Conversation not available</div>
185	                  <div className="mt-0.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] break-all opacity-80">{error}</div>
186	                  <a className="mt-1 inline-block underline" {...linkProps({ kind: 'home' })}>
187	                    Start a new run
188	                  </a>
189	                </div>
190	              </div>
191	            </div>
192	          ) : (
193	            <TranscriptView
194	              transcript={transcript}
195	              diffs={rv.state.diffs}
196	              maxSteps={rv.state.maxSteps || scenario?.max_steps || 20}
197	              loading={!!selected && rv.loading}
198	              loadingLabel="Loading run…"
199	              error={rv.loadError}
200	              transport={rv.transport}
201	              footer={
202	                <div className="space-y-3 pt-2">
203	                  {detail ? (
204	                    <RunSwitcher conversationId={detail.conversation.id} runs={runs} selectedId={selected?.id ?? null} />
205	                  ) : null}
206	                  {detail && runs.length === 0 ? (
207	                    <p className="text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">
208	                      No runs in this conversation yet — send the task below to start one.
209	                    </p>
210	                  ) : null}
211	                </div>
212	              }
213	            />
214	          )}
215	
216	          {detail ? (
217	            <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-border bg-background/90 px-4 py-3 backdrop-blur md:px-6">
218	              <div className="mx-auto w-full max-w-3xl space-y-2">
219	                <Composer
220	                  scenarios={scenario ? [scenario] : harness.scenarios}
221	                  scenarioId={detail.conversation.scenario_id}
222	                  onScenarioChange={() => undefined}
223	                  models={MODEL_ALLOWLIST}
224	                  model={model}
225	                  onModelChange={setModel}
226	                  seed={seed}
227	                  onSeedChange={setSeed}
228	                  prompt={prompt}
229	                  onPromptChange={setPrompt}
230	                  onSubmit={submit}
231	                  disabled={!harness.reachable || live || busy}
232	                  busy={busy}
233	                  placeholder={live ? 'A run is in progress…' : 'Edit the task and press enter to run it again in this conversation'}
234	                  hint={
235	                    live
236	                      ? 'wait for the current run to finish'
237	                      : `${detail.conversation.scenario_id} · another run in this conversation`
238	                  }
239	                />
240	                {startError ? (
241	                  <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-destructive/40 bg-destructive/10 px-3 py-2 text-[13px] text-destructive">
242	                    <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 size-4 shrink-0" aria-hidden />
243	                    <span className="break-all">Could not start the run: {startError}</span>
244	                  </div>
245	                ) : null}
246	              </div>
247	            </div>
248	          ) : null}
249	        </div>
250	
251	        <WorkspacePanel state={selected ? rv.state : null} live={live} open={workspaceOpen} onOpenChange={setWorkspaceOpen} />
252	      </div>
253	    </div>
254	  )
255	}

1	import { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react'
2	
3	import { RunStatusStrip, type TranscriptSource } from '@/components/layout/RunStatusStrip'
4	import { TopBar } from '@/components/layout/TopBar'
5	import { TranscriptView } from '@/components/transcript/TranscriptView'
6	import { WorkspacePanel, useWorkspaceOpen } from '@/components/workspace/WorkspaceColumn'
7	import type { HarnessState } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
8	import { useRunView } from '@/hooks/useRunView'
9	import { getLogger } from '@/lib/log'
10	import { navigate } from '@/lib/router'
11	import { transcriptFromViewState } from '@/lib/transcript'
12	
13	const log = getLogger('web')
14	
15	/** A run outside a conversation (harness without the SQLite store). Upgrades itself to
16	 *  /conversations/{id} when the run record turns out to carry a conversation_id. */
17	export function RunPage({ harness, runId }: { harness: HarnessState; runId: string }) {
18	  const source = useMemo(() => ({ kind: 'live', runId }) as const, [runId])
19	  const rv = useRunView(harness.client, source)
20	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
21	  const live = rv.state.status === 'running' || rv.state.status === 'queued'
22	
23	  useEffect(() => {
24	    const client = harness.client
25	    if (!client) return
26	    let cancelled = false
27	    void client
28	      .getRun(runId)
29	      .then((rec) => {
30	        if (cancelled || !rec.conversation_id) return
31	        log.info('run.upgrade', 'run belongs to a conversation; redirecting', {
32	          run_id: runId,
33	          conversation_id: rec.conversation_id,
34	        })
35	        navigate({ kind: 'conversation', id: rec.conversation_id, runId }, { replace: true })
36	      })
37	      .catch(() => undefined)
38	    return () => {
39	      cancelled = true
40	    }
41	  }, [harness.client, runId])
42	
43	  useEffect(() => {
44	    document.title = `${rv.state.scenarioId ?? 'run'} · ${runId} — Faultline`
45	  }, [rv.state.scenarioId, runId])
46	
47	  const transcript = useMemo(() => transcriptFromViewState(rv.state, live), [rv.state, live])
48	  const src: TranscriptSource = live ? (rv.transport?.mode === 'poll' ? 'poll' : 'sse') : 'record'
49	
50	  return (
51	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
52	      <TopBar
53	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Runs', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: rv.state.scenarioId ? `${rv.state.scenarioId} · ${runId}` : runId }]}
54	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
55	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
56	      >
57	        <RunStatusStrip state={rv.state} transport={rv.transport} source={src} />
58	      </TopBar>
59	      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">
60	        <div className="flex min-h-0 min-w-0 flex-1 flex-col">
61	          <TranscriptView
62	            transcript={transcript}
63	            diffs={rv.state.diffs}
64	            maxSteps={rv.state.maxSteps}
65	            loading={rv.loading}
66	            loadingLabel="Loading run…"
67	            error={rv.loadError}
68	            transport={rv.transport}
69	          />
70	        </div>
71	        <WorkspacePanel state={rv.state} live={live} open={workspaceOpen} onOpenChange={setWorkspaceOpen} />
72	      </div>
73	    </div>
74	  )
75	}

12	/** Bundled recorded run, played back at replay speed. Needs no backend. */
13	export function ReplayPage({ harness, demoId }: { harness: HarnessState; demoId: string }) {
14	  const source = useMemo(() => ({ kind: 'demo', demoId }) as const, [demoId])
15	  const rv = useRunView(harness.client, source)
16	  const [workspaceOpen, setWorkspaceOpen] = useWorkspaceOpen()
17	  const meta = DEMOS.find((d) => d.id === demoId)
18	  const live = rv.replaying
19	
20	  useEffect(() => {
21	    document.title = `replay · ${demoId} — Faultline`
22	  }, [demoId])
23	
24	  const transcript = useMemo(() => transcriptFromViewState(rv.state, live), [rv.state, live])
25	
26	  return (
27	    <div className="flex h-dvh min-h-0 flex-col">
28	      <TopBar
29	        crumbs={[{ label: 'Replays', route: { kind: 'home' } }, { label: meta?.label ?? demoId }]}
30	        onToggleWorkspace={() => setWorkspaceOpen((v) => !v)}
31	        workspaceOpen={workspaceOpen}
32	      >
33	        <RunStatusStrip state={rv.state} transport={null} source="replay" replaying={rv.replaying} />

1	/**
2	 * CodeBlock — adapted from Beautiful UI `CodeBlock` (MIT, © 2026 Shane Levine).
3	 *
4	 * Line-numbered listing, or a unified diff parsed from text with +/- colouring. The original
5	 * took pre-split rows and demo data; this one takes raw strings. No network access.
6	 */
7	
8	import { useCallback, useMemo, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
9	import { Check, Copy, FileCode } from 'lucide-react'
10	
11	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
12	
13	export interface CodeBlockProps {
14	  /** Plain listing. Ignored when `diff` is given. */
15	  code?: string
16	  /** Unified diff text (`--- a/x`, `+++ b/x`, `@@ -1,3 +1,4 @@`, then ` `, `+`, `-` lines). */
17	  diff?: string
18	  filename?: string
19	  /** Hint only: `text`/`plain`/`log` disable the light syntax tinting. */
20	  language?: string
21	  /** Scroll inside the block past this many pixels. */
22	  maxHeight?: number
23	  className?: string
24	}
25	
26	export type DiffLineKind = 'ctx' | 'add' | 'del' | 'hunk' | 'meta' | 'note'
27	
28	export interface DiffLine {
29	  kind: DiffLineKind
30	  /** Line number in the old file (deletions and context). */
31	  old: number | null
32	  /** Line number in the new file (additions and context). */
33	  cur: number | null
34	  text: string
35	}
36	
37	/** Parse unified-diff text into numbered rows. Tolerant: unknown lines become context. */
38	export function parseUnifiedDiff(unified: string): DiffLine[] {
39	  const lines = unified.replace(/\n$/, '').split('\n')
40	  const out: DiffLine[] = []
41	  let old = 0
42	  let cur = 0
43	  let inHunk = false
44	  for (const raw of lines) {
45	    if (!inHunk && (raw.startsWith('+++') || raw.startsWith('---'))) {
46	      out.push({ kind: 'meta', old: null, cur: null, text: raw })
47	      continue
48	    }
49	    const hunk = /^@@ -(\d+)(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,\d+)? @@/.exec(raw)
50	    if (hunk) {
51	      inHunk = true
52	      old = Number(hunk[1])
53	      cur = Number(hunk[2])
54	      out.push({ kind: 'hunk', old: null, cur: null, text: raw })
55	      continue
56	    }
57	    if (raw.startsWith('\\')) {
58	      out.push({ kind: 'note', old: null, cur: null, text: raw })
59	      continue
60	    }
61	    if (raw.startsWith('+')) {
62	      out.push({ kind: 'add', old: null, cur: cur++, text: raw.slice(1) })
63	      continue
64	    }
65	    if (raw.startsWith('-')) {
66	      out.push({ kind: 'del', old: old++, cur: null, text: raw.slice(1) })
67	      continue
68	    }
69	    out.push({ kind: 'ctx', old: old++, cur: cur++, text: raw.startsWith(' ') ? raw.slice(1) : raw })
70	  }
71	  return out
72	}
73	
74	const KEYWORDS = new Set([
75	  // js / ts
76	  'import', 'from', 'export', 'default', 'async', 'function', 'const', 'let', 'var', 'await',
77	  'return', 'if', 'else', 'for', 'while', 'new', 'throw', 'try', 'catch', 'null', 'true', 'false',
78	  'undefined',
79	  // python (the sandbox fixture is a Python package)
80	  'def', 'class', 'elif', 'in', 'not', 'and', 'or', 'is', 'None', 'True', 'False', 'except',
81	  'finally', 'with', 'as', 'lambda', 'pass', 'raise', 'yield',
82	])
83	
84	const TOKEN =
85	  /("(?:\\.|[^"\\])*"|'(?:\\.|[^'\\])*'|`[^`]*`|\b\d+(?:\.\d+)?\b|\b(?:import|from|export|default|async|function|const|let|var|await|return|if|else|for|while|new|throw|try|catch|null|true|false|undefined|def|class|elif|in|not|and|or|is|None|True|False|except|finally|with|as|lambda|pass|raise|yield)\b|[A-Za-z_$][\w$]*(?=\s*\())/g
86	
87	const PLAIN = new Set(['text', 'plain', 'log', 'txt'])
88	
89	function highlight(text: string, enabled: boolean): ReactNode {
90	  if (!enabled || text.length === 0) return text
91	  const nodes: ReactNode[] = []
92	  let last = 0
93	  let k = 0
94	  for (const m of text.matchAll(TOKEN)) {
95	    const idx = m.index ?? 0
96	    const t = m[0]
97	    if (idx > last) nodes.push(text.slice(last, idx))
98	    let className: string
99	    if (/^["'`]/.test(t) || /^\d/.test(t)) className = 'text-amber-300'
100	    else if (KEYWORDS.has(t)) className = 'text-sky-300'
101	    else className = 'font-medium text-ink'
102	    nodes.push(
103	      <span key={k++} className={className}>
104	        {t}
105	      </span>,
106	    )
107	    last = idx + t.length
108	  }
109	  if (last < text.length) nodes.push(text.slice(last))
110	  return nodes
111	}
112	
113	/** Deleted-line gutter bar: the original's diagonal hatch, drawn in `currentColor`. */
114	const HATCH =
115	  'repeating-linear-gradient(45deg, currentColor 0, currentColor 1.5px, transparent 1.5px, transparent 3px)'
116	
117	const ROW_TONE: Record<DiffLineKind, string> = {
118	  ctx: '',
119	  add: 'bg-emerald-500/10',
120	  del: 'bg-rose-500/10',
121	  hunk: 'bg-muted/30 text-sky-300/80',
122	  meta: 'text-ink-3',
123	  note: 'text-ink-3 italic',
124	}
125	
126	const NUM_TONE: Record<DiffLineKind, string> = {
127	  ctx: 'text-ink-3',
128	  add: 'text-emerald-300',
129	  del: 'text-rose-300',
130	  hunk: 'text-ink-3',
131	  meta: 'text-ink-3',
132	  note: 'text-ink-3',
133	}
134	
135	export function CodeBlock({ code, diff, filename, language, maxHeight, className }: CodeBlockProps) {
136	  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)
137	  const isDiff = typeof diff === 'string'
138	  const raw = isDiff ? diff : (code ?? '')
139	  const tint = !PLAIN.has((language ?? '').toLowerCase())
140	
141	  const rows = useMemo(() => (isDiff ? parseUnifiedDiff(diff) : null), [isDiff, diff])
142	  const lines = useMemo(() => (isDiff ? null : (code ?? '').replace(/\n$/, '').split('\n')), [isDiff, code])
143	
144	  const copy = useCallback(() => {
145	    if (typeof navigator === 'undefined' || !navigator.clipboard?.writeText) return
146	    navigator.clipboard
147	      .writeText(raw)
148	      .then(() => {
149	        setCopied(true)
150	        setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500)
151	      })
152	      .catch(() => {
153	        /* clipboard refused (insecure context / permission) — nothing to recover */
154	      })
155	  }, [raw])
156	
157	  const added = rows?.filter((r) => r.kind === 'add').length ?? 0
158	  const removed = rows?.filter((r) => r.kind === 'del').length ?? 0
159	  const title = filename ?? language ?? (isDiff ? 'diff' : 'output')
160	
161	  return (
162	    <div
163	      className={cn('w-full overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-line bg-surface', className)}
164	      data-slot="code-block"
165	    >
166	      <div className="flex h-9 items-center gap-2 border-b border-line px-3 text-[12px]">
167	        <span className="inline-flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1.5">
168	          <FileCode className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-ink-3" aria-hidden />
169	          <span className="truncate font-mono leading-none text-ink">{title}</span>
170	        </span>
171	        <span className="ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-2">
172	          {isDiff ? (
173	            <span
174	              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 font-mono text-[11.5px] leading-none tabular-nums"
175	              aria-label={`${added} added, ${removed} removed`}
176	            >
177	              <span className="text-emerald-300">+{added}</span>
178	              <span className="text-rose-300">−{removed}</span>
179	            </span>
180	          ) : null}
181	          <button
182	            type="button"
183	            aria-label={copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy code'}
184	            onClick={copy}
185	            className={cn(
186	              '-mr-1 flex h-6 items-center gap-1 rounded-md px-1.5 text-[11.5px] font-medium transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-muted/60 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none',
187	              copied ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-ink-3 hover:text-ink',
188	            )}
189	          >
190	            {copied ? <Check className="size-3" aria-hidden /> : <Copy className="size-3" aria-hidden />}
191	            <span aria-live="polite">{copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}</span>
192	          </button>
193	        </span>
194	      </div>
195	
196	      <div
197	        className="overflow-auto py-2 font-mono text-[12px] leading-[1.65] text-ink-2"
198	        style={maxHeight ? { maxHeight } : undefined}
199	      >
200	        <div className="relative min-w-fit">
201	          <span aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-y-0 left-10 w-px bg-line" />
202	          {rows
203	            ? rows.map((r, i) => {
204	                const num = r.kind === 'del' ? r.old : r.cur
205	                return (
206	                  <div
207	                    key={i}
208	                    data-line-kind={r.kind}
209	                    className={cn('relative grid grid-cols-[2.5rem_minmax(0,1fr)] items-start', ROW_TONE[r.kind])}
210	                  >
211	                    {r.kind === 'add' || r.kind === 'del' ? (
212	                      <span
213	                        aria-hidden
214	                        className={cn(
215	                          'absolute inset-y-0 left-0 w-[3px]',
216	                          r.kind === 'add' ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'text-rose-400',
217	                        )}
218	                        style={r.kind === 'del' ? { background: HATCH } : undefined}
219	                      />
220	                    ) : null}
221	                    <span className={cn('pr-2 text-right text-[11px] select-none', NUM_TONE[r.kind])}>
222	                      {num ?? ''}
223	                    </span>
224	                    <code className="pr-3 pl-2.5 break-words whitespace-pre-wrap">
225	                      {r.kind === 'ctx' || r.kind === 'add' || r.kind === 'del'
226	                        ? highlight(r.text, tint)
227	                        : r.text}
228	                    </code>
229	                  </div>
230	                )
231	              })
232	            : lines!.map((line, i) => (
233	                <div key={i} data-line-kind="code" className="grid grid-cols-[2.5rem_minmax(0,1fr)] items-start">
234	                  <span className="pr-2 text-right text-[11px] text-ink-3 select-none">{i + 1}</span>
235	                  <code className="pr-3 pl-2.5 break-words whitespace-pre-wrap">{highlight(line, tint)}</code>
236	                </div>
237	              ))}
238	        </div>
239	      </div>
240	    </div>
241	  )
242	}

1	/**
2	 * ThinkingTrace — adapted from Beautiful UI `ThinkingState` ("Steps" variant; MIT, © 2026 Shane
3	 * Levine).
4	 *
5	 * A collapsible per-step trace. The header shimmers while the step is in flight and settles when
6	 * it is done; the body lists the step's tool calls down a thin timeline rail (a spinner on the
7	 * call still running, a check or cross once it has a result), an optional thinking paragraph
8	 * above, and whatever the parent passes as children (ToolCallChips) below.
9	 *
10	 * The scripted stage timers are gone: `active` / `done` come from the run reducer.
11	 */
12	
13	import { useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
14	import { Check, ChevronDown, Sparkles, Terminal, X } from 'lucide-react'
15	
16	import { FaultFiredBadge, RecoveredBadge } from '@/components/FaultBadge'
17	import { oneLine } from '@/lib/format'
18	import type { ToolCallView } from '@/lib/reducer'
19	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
20	
21	import { summariseCall, TOOL_ICON, toolLabel } from './ToolCallChips'
22	
23	export interface ThinkingTraceProps {
24	  step: number
25	  maxSteps?: number
26	  calls: ToolCallView[]
27	  /** Prose the agent produced in this step, shown above the call rows. */
28	  thinking?: string | null
29	  /** The step is in flight: shimmer header, spinner on the call without a result. */
30	  active: boolean
31	  /** The step has settled. */
32	  done: boolean
33	  /** Initial open state; defaults to open while `active`. Users can toggle regardless. */
34	  defaultOpen?: boolean
35	  /** Rendered inside the expanded body, under the rows. */
36	  children?: ReactNode
37	}
38	
39	function plural(n: number, word: string): string {
40	  return `${n} ${word}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`
41	}
42	
43	function statusText(active: boolean, done: boolean, n: number): string {
44	  if (active) return n === 0 ? 'thinking' : `running ${plural(n, 'tool call')}`
45	  if (done) return n === 0 ? 'no tool calls' : plural(n, 'tool call')
46	  return 'pending'
47	}
48	
49	function Glyph({ call, active }: { call: ToolCallView; active: boolean }) {
50	  if (!call.result) {
51	    return active ? (
52	      <span
53	        className="bui-spin size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line-strong border-t-ink-2"
54	        aria-label="running"
55	        role="img"
56	      />
57	    ) : (
58	      <span className="size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line" aria-label="pending" role="img" />
59	    )
60	  }
61	  return call.result.isError ? (
62	    <span role="img" aria-label="error" className="flex shrink-0">
63	      <X className="size-3.5 text-rose-300" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
64	    </span>
65	  ) : (
66	    <span role="img" aria-label="ok" className="flex shrink-0">
67	      <Check className="size-3.5 text-ink-3" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
68	    </span>
69	  )
70	}
71	
72	export function ThinkingTrace({
73	  step,
74	  maxSteps,
75	  calls,
76	  thinking,
77	  active,
78	  done,
79	  defaultOpen,
80	  children,
81	}: ThinkingTraceProps) {
82	  const [manual, setManual] = useState<boolean | null>(null)
83	  const expanded = manual ?? defaultOpen ?? active
84	  const panelId = useId()
85	
86	  const n = calls.length
87	  const faults = calls.filter((c) => c.fault).length
88	  const recovered = calls.filter((c) => c.fault && c.recovered).length
89	  const errors = calls.filter((c) => c.result?.isError).length
90	  const title = maxSteps ? `Step ${step} of ${maxSteps}` : `Step ${step}`
91	
92	  return (
93	    <div className="flex w-full flex-col" data-slot="thinking-trace" data-step={step}>
94	      <button
95	        type="button"
96	        aria-expanded={expanded}
97	        aria-controls={panelId}
98	        onClick={() => setManual(!expanded)}
99	        className="-mx-1.5 flex w-fit max-w-full items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1.5 py-1 text-left transition-colors duration-100 hover:bg-muted/40 focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none"
100	      >
101	        <Sparkles
102	          className={cn('size-4 shrink-0 transition-colors duration-200', active ? 'text-ink-2' : 'text-ink-3')}
103	          fill="currentColor"
104	          strokeWidth={0}
105	          aria-hidden
106	        />
107	        <span className="text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink">{title}</span>
108	        <span role="status" className="contents">
109	          {active ? (
110	            <span className="bui-shimmer text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink-2">
111	              {statusText(active, done, n)}
112	            </span>
113	          ) : (
114	            <span className="bui-fade-in text-[13px] font-medium whitespace-nowrap text-ink-2">
115	              {statusText(active, done, n)}
116	            </span>
117	          )}
118	        </span>
119	        {faults > 0 ? (
120	          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-amber-300 tabular-nums">{plural(faults, 'fault')}</span>
121	        ) : null}
122	        {recovered > 0 ? (
123	          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-emerald-300 tabular-nums">{recovered} recovered</span>
124	        ) : null}
125	        {errors > 0 && errors !== faults ? (
126	          <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-rose-300 tabular-nums">{plural(errors, 'error')}</span>
127	        ) : null}
128	        <ChevronDown
129	          className={cn('size-3.5 shrink-0 text-ink-3 transition-transform duration-300', expanded && 'rotate-180')}
130	          aria-hidden
131	        />
132	      </button>
133	
134	      <div
135	        id={panelId}
136	        aria-hidden={!expanded}
137	        inert={!expanded}
138	        className={cn(
139	          'grid motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] motion-safe:duration-400 motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]',
140	          expanded ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100' : 'grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0',
141	        )}
142	      >
143	        <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
144	          <div className="relative mt-1 ml-[7px] pl-4">
145	            <span aria-hidden className="absolute top-0 bottom-1 left-0 w-px bg-line" />
146	            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1 py-1">
147	              {thinking ? (
148	                <p className="px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink-2">
149	                  {thinking}
150	                </p>
151	              ) : null}
152	
153	              {calls.map((call, i) => {
154	                const Icon = TOOL_ICON[call.tool] ?? Terminal
155	                return (
156	                  <div
157	                    key={call.toolUseId}
158	                    className="bui-fade-up flex min-h-7 w-full min-w-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1 rounded-md px-1.5 py-0.5"
159	                    style={{ animationDelay: `${active ? 0 : Math.min(i, 8) * 60}ms` }}
160	                    data-slot="trace-row"
161	                  >
162	                    <Glyph call={call} active={active} />
163	                    <Icon className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-ink-3" aria-hidden />
164	                    <span className="shrink-0 text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{toolLabel(call.tool)}</span>
165	                    <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3">
166	                      {oneLine(summariseCall(call), 140)}
167	                    </span>
168	                    {call.fault ? <FaultFiredBadge kind={call.fault.kind} /> : null}
169	                    {call.recovered ? <RecoveredBadge /> : null}
170	                  </div>
171	                )
172	              })}
173	
174	              {active && n === 0 ? (
175	                <div className="flex min-h-7 items-center gap-2 px-1.5 text-[12.5px] text-ink-3">
176	                  <span className="bui-spin size-3 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line-strong border-t-ink-2" />
177	                  waiting for the model…
178	                </div>
179	              ) : null}
180	
181	              {children ? <div className="mt-1 min-w-0">{children}</div> : null}
182	            </div>
183	          </div>
184	        </div>
185	      </div>
186	    </div>
187	  )
188	}

1	/**
2	 * ScoreRows — adapted from Beautiful UI `TaskRows` ("Capsules" variant; MIT, © 2026 Shane
3	 * Levine).
4	 *
5	 * A score headline, then one capsule per grading check (round pass/fail badge, id, weight,
6	 * status pill) that expands to the grader's detail. A final capsule holds the pytest summary
7	 * and expands to the raw output in a CodeBlock. The demo's staged running/failed sequence was
8	 * removed — an evaluation is always settled.
9	 */
10	
11	import { useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
12	import { Check, ChevronDown, X } from 'lucide-react'
13	
14	import type { Check as CheckResult, EvaluateResponse } from '@/lib/types'
15	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
16	
17	import { CodeBlock } from './CodeBlock'
18	
19	export interface ScoreRowsProps {
20	  evaluation: EvaluateResponse
21	}
22	
23	export function scoreTone(score: number): string {
24	  if (score >= 85) return 'text-emerald-300'
25	  if (score >= 50) return 'text-amber-300'
26	  return 'text-rose-300'
27	}
28	
29	function RoundBadge({ ok }: { ok: boolean }) {
30	  return (
31	    <span
32	      className={cn(
33	        'bui-pop-in flex size-5.5 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-white',
34	        ok ? 'bg-emerald-500' : 'bg-rose-500',
35	      )}
36	      role="img"
37	      aria-label={ok ? 'pass' : 'fail'}
38	    >
39	      {ok ? <Check className="size-3" strokeWidth={3.5} aria-hidden /> : <X className="size-3" strokeWidth={3.5} aria-hidden />}
40	    </span>
41	  )
42	}
43	
44	function Pill({ ok, children }: { ok: boolean; children: ReactNode }) {
45	  return (
46	    <span
47	      className={cn(
48	        'inline-flex h-5.5 shrink-0 items-center rounded-full px-2 text-[11.5px] font-medium',
49	        ok ? 'bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300',
50	      )}
51	    >
52	      {children}
53	    </span>
54	  )
55	}
56	
57	function Capsule({
58	  ok,
59	  label,
60	  mono = false,
61	  amount,
62	  pill,
63	  index,
64	  open,
65	  onToggle,
66	  children,
67	}: {
68	  ok: boolean
69	  label: string
70	  mono?: boolean
71	  amount: string
72	  pill: string
73	  index: number
74	  open: boolean
75	  onToggle: () => void
76	  children: ReactNode
77	}) {
78	  const panelId = useId()
79	  return (
80	    <div
81	      className="bui-fade-up self-stretch overflow-hidden border border-line bg-surface transition-[border-radius,background-color] duration-300 hover:bg-muted/30"
82	      style={{ borderRadius: open ? 14 : 22, animationDelay: `${index * 80}ms` }}
83	      data-slot="score-row"
84	      data-ok={ok}
85	    >
86	      <button
87	        type="button"
88	        aria-expanded={open}
89	        aria-controls={panelId}
90	        onClick={onToggle}
91	        className="flex h-11 w-full items-center gap-2.5 px-2.5 text-left focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-inset"
92	      >
93	        <span className="flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center">
94	          <RoundBadge ok={ok} />
95	        </span>
96	        <span className={cn('min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[13px] font-medium text-ink', mono && 'font-mono text-[12.5px]')}>
97	          {label}
98	        </span>
99	        <span className="shrink-0 text-[12px] text-ink-2 tabular-nums">{amount}</span>
100	        <Pill ok={ok}>{pill}</Pill>
101	        <span aria-hidden className="-ml-1 flex size-7 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-ink-3">
102	          <ChevronDown className={cn('size-[15px] transition-transform duration-300', open && 'rotate-180')} />
103	        </span>
104	      </button>
105	
106	      <div
107	        id={panelId}
108	        aria-hidden={!open}
109	        inert={!open}
110	        className={cn(
111	          'grid motion-safe:transition-[grid-template-rows,opacity] motion-safe:duration-300 motion-safe:ease-[cubic-bezier(0.23,1,0.32,1)]',
112	          open ? 'grid-rows-[1fr] opacity-100' : 'grid-rows-[0fr] opacity-0',
113	        )}
114	      >
115	        <div className="min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
116	          <div className="mb-2.5 grid grid-cols-[24px_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-2.5 px-2.5">
117	            <span aria-hidden className="mx-auto h-full w-px bg-line" />
118	            <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-1.5">{children}</div>
119	          </div>
120	        </div>
121	      </div>
122	    </div>
123	  )
124	}
125	
126	function CheckBody({ check }: { check: CheckResult }) {
127	  return (
128	    <div className="bui-fade-up flex items-start justify-between gap-3" style={{ animationDelay: '120ms' }}>
129	      <span className="min-w-0 text-[12px] leading-relaxed break-words text-ink-2">{check.detail || '—'}</span>
130	      <span className="shrink-0 font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">{check.ok ? 'ok' : 'fail'}</span>
131	    </div>
132	  )
133	}
134	
135	export function ScoreRows({ evaluation }: ScoreRowsProps) {
136	  const tests = evaluation.tests ?? { passed: 0, failed: 0, errors: 0, output: '' }
137	  const checks = evaluation.checks ?? []
138	  const okChecks = checks.filter((c) => c.ok).length
139	  const testsOk = tests.failed === 0 && tests.errors === 0
140	  const [open, setOpen] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({})
141	  const toggle = (key: string) => setOpen((cur) => ({ ...cur, [key]: !cur[key] }))
142	
143	  return (
144	    <div className="flex w-full flex-col gap-2" data-slot="score-rows">
145	      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-3 gap-y-1 px-1">
146	        <span className={cn('font-mono text-3xl tabular-nums', scoreTone(evaluation.score))} aria-label="score">
147	          {Math.round(evaluation.score)}
148	        </span>
149	        <span className="text-[12.5px] text-ink-3">/ 100</span>
150	        <Pill ok={evaluation.passed}>{evaluation.passed ? 'Passed' : 'Failed'}</Pill>
151	        <span className="font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">
152	          {tests.passed} passed · {tests.failed} failed · {tests.errors} errors
153	        </span>
154	        <span className="font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-3 tabular-nums">
155	          checks {okChecks}/{checks.length}
156	        </span>
157	      </div>
158	
159	      {checks.map((c, i) => (
160	        <Capsule
161	          key={c.id}
162	          ok={c.ok}
163	          label={c.id}
164	          mono
165	          amount={`weight ${c.weight}`}
166	          pill={c.ok ? 'Passed' : 'Failed'}
167	          index={i}
168	          open={!!open[`check:${c.id}`]}
169	          onToggle={() => toggle(`check:${c.id}`)}
170	        >
171	          <CheckBody check={c} />
172	        </Capsule>
173	      ))}
174	
175	      <Capsule
176	        ok={testsOk}
177	        label="pytest"
178	        amount={`${tests.passed} passed · ${tests.failed} failed · ${tests.errors} errors`}
179	        pill={testsOk ? 'Passed' : 'Failed'}
180	        index={checks.length}
181	        open={!!open.tests}
182	        onToggle={() => toggle('tests')}
183	      >
184	        <CodeBlock
185	          code={tests.output || '(no output)'}
186	          filename="python -m pytest -q"
187	          language="log"
188	          maxHeight={240}
189	        />
190	      </Capsule>
191	
192	      <p className="px-1 text-[11px] text-ink-3">
193	        score = 60 × tests_pass + 40 × weighted recovery checks (services/sandbox-env/GRADING.md)
194	      </p>
195	    </div>
196	  )
197	}

7	describe('ScoreRows', () => {

11	    expect(screen.getByLabelText('score').textContent).toBe('72')
12	    expect(within(headline).getByText('6 passed · 0 failed · 0 errors')).toBeTruthy()
13	    expect(within(headline).getByText('Passed')).toBeTruthy()

15	
16	    const rows = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="score-row"]')
17	    expect(rows).toHaveLength(4) // 3 checks + pytest
18	    expect(rows[0]!.getAttribute('data-ok')).toBe('true')
19	    expect(rows[1]!.getAttribute('data-ok')).toBe('false')
20	    expect(within(rows[1]!).getByText('no_duplicate_entry')).toBeTruthy()
21	    expect(within(rows[1]!).getByRole('img', { name: 'fail' })).toBeTruthy()
22	    expect(within(rows[1]!).getByText('Failed')).toBeTruthy()
23	    expect(within(rows[0]!).getByRole('img', { name: 'pass' })).toBeTruthy()
24	    expect(within(rows[3]!).getByText('pytest')).toBeTruthy()
25	  })
26	
27	  it('expands a check to its detail and weight', () => {
28	    render(<ScoreRows evaluation={evaluation} />)
29	    const rows = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="score-row"]')
30	    const toggle = within(rows[1]!).getByRole('button')
31	    expect(toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false')
32	    fireEvent.click(toggle)
33	    expect(toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true')
34	    expect(within(rows[1]!).getByText('two lines match ^## \\[0\\.2\\.0\\]')).toBeTruthy()
35	    expect(within(rows[1]!).getByText('weight 2')).toBeTruthy()
36	    expect(within(rows[1]!).getByText('fail')).toBeTruthy()
37	    expect(within(rows[2]!).getByText('weight 1')).toBeTruthy()
38	  })
39	
40	  it('expands the pytest row to the raw output in a code block', () => {
41	    render(<ScoreRows evaluation={evaluation} />)
42	    const rows = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="score-row"]')
43	    fireEvent.click(within(rows[3]!).getByRole('button'))
44	    expect(within(rows[3]!).getByText('python -m pytest -q')).toBeTruthy()
45	    expect(within(rows[3]!).getByText(/6 passed in 0.09s/)).toBeTruthy()
46	    expect(rows[3]!.querySelectorAll('[data-line-kind="code"]')).toHaveLength(2)
47	  })
48	
49	  it('shows a failed headline when tests fail', () => {
50	    render(
51	      <ScoreRows
52	        evaluation={{
53	          ...evaluation,
54	          score: 12,
55	          passed: false,

57	        }}
58	      />,
59	    )
60	    expect(screen.getByLabelText('score').className).toContain('rose')
61	    const headline = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="score-rows"] > div')!
62	    expect(within(headline).getByText('4 passed · 2 failed · 0 errors')).toBeTruthy()
63	    expect(within(headline).getByText('Failed')).toBeTruthy()
64	    const pytest = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="score-row"]')[3]!
65	    expect(pytest.getAttribute('data-ok')).toBe('false')
66	    expect(within(pytest).getByText('Failed')).toBeTruthy()
67	  })
68	})

Tool reply2026-09-12T22:33:37.853Z
1	/**
2	 * LoadingState — adapted from Beautiful UI `LoadingState` ("Drive" variant; MIT, © 2026 Shane
3	 * Levine). A 3×3 pixel grid pulses in a chevron sweep next to a shimmering label and a running
4	 * elapsed clock. The video "Surfer" variant was dropped.
5	 */
6	
7	import { useEffect, useState, type CSSProperties } from 'react'
8	
9	import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'
10	
11	export interface LoadingStateProps {
12	  label: string
13	  sublabel?: string
14	  className?: string
15	}
16	
17	/** Delay per cell so the grid lights up as a chevron pointing right. */
18	const CHEVRON = Array.from({ length: 9 }, (_, i) => {
19	  const r = Math.floor(i / 3)
20	  const c = i % 3
21	  return (c + Math.abs(r - 1)) * 90
22	})
23	const DUR_MS = 650
24	
25	function LoaderGrid() {
26	  return (
27	    <span aria-hidden className="grid shrink-0 grid-cols-[repeat(3,4px)] gap-[1.5px]">
28	      {CHEVRON.map((delay, i) => (
29	        <span
30	          key={i}
31	          className="bui-pixel size-[4px] rounded-[1px] bg-ink opacity-15"
32	          style={{ '--bui-delay': `${delay}ms`, '--bui-dur': `${DUR_MS}ms` } as CSSProperties}
33	        />
34	      ))}
35	    </span>
36	  )
37	}
38	
39	function useElapsed(): string {
40	  const [ds, setDs] = useState(0)
41	  useEffect(() => {
42	    const t = setInterval(() => setDs((d) => d + 1), 100)
43	    return () => clearInterval(t)
44	  }, [])
45	  const total = ds / 10
46	  if (total < 60) return `${total.toFixed(1)}s`
47	  return `${Math.floor(total / 60)}m ${(total % 60).toFixed(1)}s`
48	}
49	
50	export function LoadingState({ label, sublabel, className }: LoadingStateProps) {
51	  const elapsed = useElapsed()
52	  return (
53	    <div role="status" aria-live="polite" className={cn('flex w-fit flex-col gap-1', className)}>

export function AppSidebar({
  harness,
  conversations,
  route,
  identity,
}: {
  harness: HarnessState
  conversations: ConversationsState
  route: Route
  identity: IdentityState
}) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)
  const groups = useMemo(() => groupByDay(conversations.items), [conversations.items])
  const activeConversation = route.kind === 'conversation' ? route.id : null
  const activeReplay = route.kind === 'replay' ? route.demoId : null
  const userId = identity.userId ?? null

  const copyId = async () => {
    if (!userId) return
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(userId)
      setCopied(true)
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1500)
    } catch (err) {
      log.warn('identity.copy_failed', 'clipboard unavailable', { error: String(err) })
    }
  }

  const resetIdentity = () => {
    identity.reset()
    log.info('identity.reset', 'minted a new browser user id; reloading')
    window.location.assign('/')
  }

  return (
    <Sidebar collapsible="icon" variant="sidebar">
      <SidebarHeader>
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <SidebarMenuButton
              size="lg"
              tooltip="Faultline"
              render={<a {...linkProps({ kind: 'home' })} />}
              className="data-[state=open]:bg-sidebar-accent"
            >
              <div className="flex aspect-square size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-brand text-brand-ink">
                <GitBranchPlus className="size-4" aria-hidden />
              </div>
              <div className="grid flex-1 text-left leading-tight">
                <span className="truncate font-medium">Faultline</span>
                <span className="truncate text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">agent failure lab</span>
              </div>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <SidebarMenuButton
              tooltip="New run"
              isActive={route.kind === 'home'}
              render={<a {...linkProps({ kind: 'home' })} />}
            >
              <Plus aria-hidden />
              <span>New run</span>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarHeader>

      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupLabel>Conversations</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            {conversations.loading && conversations.items.length === 0 ? (
              <SidebarMenu>
                {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
                  <SidebarMenuItem key={i}>
                    <SidebarMenuSkeleton showIcon />
                  </SidebarMenuItem>
                ))}
              </SidebarMenu>
            ) : conversations.available === false ? (
              <p className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground group-data-[collapsible=icon]:hidden">
                History unavailable: this harness build has no <code className="font-mono">/conversations</code>{' '}
                routes yet. Runs still work; they will appear here once the SQLite store is deployed.
              </p>
            ) : conversations.items.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="px-2 py-1 text-[11px] leading-snug text-muted-foreground group-data-[collapsible=icon]:hidden">
                {conversations.error
                  ? `Could not load conversations: ${conversations.error}`
                  : 'No conversations yet for this browser. Start a run to create one.'}
              </p>
            ) : (
              groups.map((g) => (
                <div key={g.label} className="mb-1">
                  <div className="px-2 pt-1 pb-0.5 text-[10px] tracking-wide text-muted-foreground/70 uppercase group-data-[collapsible=icon]:hidden">
                    {g.label}
                  </div>
                  <SidebarMenu>
                    {g.items.map((c) => (
                      <ConversationRow key={c.id} c={c} active={c.id === activeConversation} />
                    ))}
                  </SidebarMenu>
                </div>
              ))
            )}
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>

        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupLabel>Replays</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              {DEMOS.map((d) => (
                <SidebarMenuItem key={d.id}>
                  <SidebarMenuButton
                    tooltip={`Replay ${d.label}`}
                    isActive={d.id === activeReplay}
                    render={<a {...linkProps({ kind: 'replay', demoId: d.id })} />}
                  >
                    <Rewind aria-hidden />
                    <span className="truncate">{d.label}</span>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              ))}
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>

      <SidebarFooter>
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenu>
              <DropdownMenuTrigger
                render={
                  <SidebarMenuButton
                    size="lg"
                    tooltip="Browser identity"
                    className="data-[popup-open]:bg-sidebar-accent data-[state=open]:bg-sidebar-accent"
                  >
                    <Avatar className="size-8 rounded-lg">
                      <AvatarFallback className="rounded-lg font-mono text-[11px] uppercase">
                        {userId ? userId.slice(2, 4) : '??'}
                      </AvatarFallback>
                    </Avatar>
                    <div className="grid flex-1 text-left leading-tight">
                      <span className="truncate text-[13px]">Browser user</span>
                      <span className="truncate font-mono text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                        {shortId(userId)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                }
              />
              <DropdownMenuContent side="top" align="start" className="w-72">
                <DropdownMenuLabel className="space-y-0.5">
                  <div className="text-xs font-medium">Anonymous browser identity</div>
                  <div className="font-mono text-[11px] break-all text-muted-foreground">{userId ?? '—'}</div>
                  <div className="text-[11px] font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                    Minted in this browser, kept in localStorage with a cookie mirror, sent as{' '}
                    <code className="font-mono">X-Faultline-User</code>. Conversations are scoped to it.
                  </div>
                </DropdownMenuLabel>
                <DropdownMenuSeparator />
                <DropdownMenuItem onClick={() => void copyId()}>
                  {copied ? <Check aria-hidden /> : <Copy aria-hidden />}
                  {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy user id'}
                </DropdownMenuItem>
                <DropdownMenuItem onClick={resetIdentity}>
                  <RotateCcw aria-hidden />
                  Reset identity (new id, empty history)
                </DropdownMenuItem>
                <DropdownMenuSeparator />
                <DropdownMenuLabel className="space-y-0.5 font-normal">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
                    <span
                      className={`inline-block size-1.5 rounded-full ${harness.reachable ? 'bg-emerald-400' : 'bg-rose-400'}`}
                    />
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                      harness {harness.reachable ? 'reachable' : 'unreachable'}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  {harness.health?.has_provider_key === false ? (
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-emerald-300">
                      <ShieldCheck className="size-3" aria-hidden /> no provider key on the api function
                    </div>
                  ) : null}
                  <div className="font-mono text-[10px] break-all text-muted-foreground/80">
                    {harness.base ?? ''}
                  </div>
                </DropdownMenuLabel>
              </DropdownMenuContent>
            </DropdownMenu>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarFooter>
      <SidebarRail />
    </Sidebar>
  )
}

Tool reply2026-09-12T22:34:00.320Z
/** Small presentation helpers. No React, no side effects — safe to unit test. */

export function fmtDuration(ms: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (ms === null || ms === undefined || !Number.isFinite(ms)) return '—'
  if (ms < 1000) return `${Math.round(ms)} ms`
  const s = ms / 1000
  if (s < 60) return `${s.toFixed(s < 10 ? 1 : 0)} s`
  const m = Math.floor(s / 60)
  const rem = Math.round(s % 60)
  return `${m}m ${String(rem).padStart(2, '0')}s`
}

export function fmtBytes(n: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (n === null || n === undefined || !Number.isFinite(n)) return '—'
  if (n < 1024) return `${n} B`
  if (n < 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(1)} KB`
  return `${(n / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1)} MB`
}

export function fmtTokens(n: number): string {
  if (n < 1000) return String(n)
  return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`
}

export function fmtTime(iso: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!iso) return '—'
  const t = Date.parse(iso)
  if (Number.isNaN(t)) return '—'
  return new Date(t).toISOString().slice(11, 23) + 'Z'
}

/** One-line preview of a command or path for a collapsed card header. */
export function oneLine(s: string, max = 120): string {
  const flat = s.replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim()
  return flat.length <= max ? flat : `${flat.slice(0, max - 1)}…`
}

export const FAULT_LABEL: Record<string, string> = {
  missing_file: 'missing_file',
  denied_write: 'denied_write',
  ack_lost: 'ack_lost',
}

export const FAULT_BLURB: Record<string, string> = {
  missing_file: 'A read was answered with ENOENT — the file is gone, or the filesystem is flaky.',
  denied_write: 'A write was refused with EACCES and nothing was written.',
  ack_lost: 'The write landed, but the response timed out — the agent cannot know whether it applied.',
}

/** First 7 hex chars of a sha256, for a file-list column. */
export function shortSha(sha: string | null | undefined): string {
  return sha ? sha.slice(0, 7) : ''
}

---App.tsx---
import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import { AppShell } from '@/components/layout/AppShell'
import { useConversations } from '@/hooks/useConversations'
import { useHarness } from '@/hooks/useHarness'
import { useIdentity } from '@/hooks/useIdentity'
import { useRoute } from '@/lib/router'
import { ConversationPage } from '@/pages/ConversationPage'
import { HomePage } from '@/pages/HomePage'
import { ReplayPage } from '@/pages/ReplayPage'
import { RunPage } from '@/pages/RunPage'

/**
 * Routes (path based, no router dependency — see src/lib/router.ts):
 *   /                        new run (composer + scenarios)
 *   /conversations/:id       a conversation loaded from the SQLite store via REST; ?run=<id> picks a run
 *   /runs/:id                a run outside a conversation (harness without the store)
 *   /replay/:demoId          bundled recorded run
 */
export default function App() {
  const route = useRoute()
  const identity = useIdentity()
  const harness = useHarness()
  const conversations = useConversations(harness.client)

  let page: React.ReactNode
  switch (route.kind) {
    case 'conversation':
      page = (
        <ConversationPage
          key={route.id}
          harness={harness}
          conversations={conversations}
          conversationId={route.id}
          runId={route.runId ?? null}
        />
      )
      break
    case 'run':
      page = <RunPage key={route.runId} harness={harness} runId={route.runId} />
      break
    case 'replay':
      page = <ReplayPage key={route.demoId} harness={harness} demoId={route.demoId} />
      break
    default:
      page = <HomePage harness={harness} conversations={conversations} />
  }

  return (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <AppShell harness={harness} conversations={conversations} route={route} identity={identity}>
        {page}
      </AppShell>
    </TooltipProvider>
  )
}

function row(tool: string, index = 0): HTMLElement {
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(`[data-slot="tool-call"][data-tool="${tool}"]`)
  return rows[index]!
}

/** The always-visible header line of a row (the expandable panel is its sibling). */
function header(r: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {
  return r.firstElementChild as HTMLElement
}

function toggleOf(r: HTMLElement): HTMLElement {
  return header(r).querySelector('button[aria-expanded]')!
}
