/**
 * Realistic fixtures for the bui component tests, shaped like public/demo/lost-ack.json folded
 * through the reducer. Results go through `parseToolResult` so the derived fields match what a
 * live run produces.
 */

import { parseToolResult, type ToolCallView } from '@/lib/reducer'
import type { EvaluateResponse, FileDiff, FileEntry, Scenario } from '@/lib/types'

function call(
  seq: number,
  step: number,
  tool: string,
  input: Record<string, unknown>,
  over: Partial<ToolCallView> = {},
): ToolCallView {
  const path = typeof input.path === 'string' ? (input.path as string) : tool === 'list_dir' ? '.' : null
  const command = typeof input.command === 'string' ? (input.command as string) : null
  return {
    seq,
    step,
    toolUseId: `tu_${String(seq + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`,
    tool,
    input,
    path,
    command,
    mutating: tool === 'write_file',
    read: tool === 'read_file' || tool === 'list_dir',
    result: null,
    fault: null,
    recovered: false,
    ...over,
  }
}

/**
 * The ten-second reliability read of a run, as data.
 *
 * Contract (ARCHITECTURE.md §5.4, docs/error-taxonomy.md): every field here comes from structured
 * event fields — `error_class`, `fault.fired`, `interruption`, `run.resumed`, the evaluate ledger,
 * `episode.evaluated`, `workspace.diff` — never from parsing an error message. Anything the record
 * does not carry is `unknown`, and the raw payloads behind every panel are kept for an expander.
 */

import type { ErrorClass, ErrorLayer, ErrorOrigin, Interruption, RunStatus } from './types'

export type PanelStatus = 'ok' | 'warn' | 'fail' | 'unknown' | 'pending'

export type PanelKey = 'task' | 'fault' | 'execution' | 'recovery' | 'result'

/** A raw payload shown under "details": the event(s) or record fields a panel was built from. */
export interface RawRef {
  label: string
  json: unknown
}

export interface Fact {
  label: string
  value: string
  tone?: PanelStatus
  /** Plain-language explanation shown in a tooltip / expander (e.g. what a code means). */
  hint?: string
}

export interface VerdictPanel {
  key: PanelKey
  status: PanelStatus
  /** The one line a reviewer reads in ten seconds (≤ ~70 chars). */
  headline: string
  /** One or two plain-language sentences. */
  plain: string
  facts: Fact[]
  raw: RawRef[]
}

/** How a failed/faulted call actually ended, and where that knowledge comes from. */
export type ExecutionOutcome = 'executed' | 'failed' | 'not_executed' | 'unknown'
export type ExecutionSource = 'outcome' | 'error_class' | 'ledger' | 'exit_code' | 'none'

export interface FollowUp {
  step: number
  tool: string
  toolUseId: string
  kind: 'read' | 'write' | 'other'
  ok: boolean | null
}

/** One failure the run hit: an injected/staged fault, a real error on a call, or an interruption. */
export interface Incident {
  id: string
  step: number | null
  tool: string | null
  toolUseId: string | null
  path: string | null
  origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown'
  layer: ErrorLayer | null
  code: string | null
  /** ErrorClass.label verbatim when present, else the fixed label for (origin, kind) from the taxonomy, else null. */
  label: string | null
  kind: string | null
  /** interruption.planned — a deliberate real failure (harness_faults). null when not an interruption. */
  planned: boolean | null
  execution: ExecutionOutcome
  executionSource: ExecutionSource
  /** error_class.side_effect_applied, or the ledger's answer once evaluate released it. */
  sideEffectApplied: boolean | null
  outcomeKnown: boolean | null
  /** Later calls touching the same path, in order — the agent's recovery actions. */
  followUps: FollowUp[]
  errorClass: ErrorClass | null
  interruption: Interruption | null
  /** tool.result.data.attempts — harness-level retries of this call (1 = none); null when absent. */
  attempts?: number | null
  /** fault.fired.data.description — one plain sentence from the environment, when present. */
  description?: string | null
  raw: RawRef[]
}

export interface GradingCheck {
  id: string
  ok: boolean
  weight: number
  /** Plain language for the check id (src/lib/checks.ts), falling back to the id. */
  description: string
  detail: string
}

export interface Grading {
  /** true once episode.evaluated arrived */
  available: boolean
  /** run.finished.data.evaluation_status when present ("ok" | "failed" | "skipped"). */
  evaluationStatus?: string | null
  score: number | null
  tests: { passed: number; failed: number; errors: number } | null
  checks: GradingCheck[]
  /** Why grading did not happen (status unevaluated / interrupted / error): the ErrorClass label. */
  blockedBy: string | null
}

export interface SandboxSummary {
  episodeId: string | null
  /** episode.reset.data.sandbox_id / episode.sandbox / tool.result.data.sandbox.id (short form), when present. */
  sandboxId?: string | null
  /** Last known liveness from tool.result.data.sandbox.alive / episode.sandbox.status; null when never reported. */
  alive?: boolean | null
  /** episode.reset.data.attempt — >1 when the harness re-provisioned the workspace. */
  attempt?: number | null
  workerGeneration: number
  resumes: number
  /** true = an interruption/error_class with layer "sandbox" was observed; false = the run was graded inside it; null = cannot say. */
  lost: boolean | null
  lostAtStep: number | null
}

export interface DiffSummary {
  files: number
  added: number
  removed: number
  paths: string[]
}

export interface Verdict {
  status: RunStatus | null
  live: boolean
  panels: Record<PanelKey, VerdictPanel>
  incidents: Incident[]
  sandbox: SandboxSummary
  grading: Grading
  diff: DiffSummary | null
}

### 5.3 Event stream

Event types and payloads are defined in `faultline_common.schemas.Event` (`run.started`, `episode.reset`,
`turn.thinking`, `turn.text`, `tool.call`, `tool.result`, `llm.call`, `fault.fired`, `workspace.diff`,
`episode.evaluated`, `run.finished`, `log`). `Event.id` is the 0-based sequence within the run and doubles
as the SSE id, the `events.seq` primary key, and the resume cursor. Events are produced by exactly one
writer per run (`run_episode`) and are never edited.

### 5.4 Reliability event contract (confirmed with the harness lead 2026-09-12 ~22:45 EDT)

Canonical: `packages/common/faultline_common/schemas.py` (the `Event` docstring lists every data
shape), labels in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`, injector behaviour in `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md`.
The UI is a ten-second reliability read — *task → fault (simulated or real, which layer) → did the
command execute (executed / failed / not executed / unknown are four different answers) → recovery
(agent actions vs harness retries; did the sandbox survive) → diff + independent checks* — and it
**never derives an outcome from error text**: anything absent renders as "unknown" with the raw
payload expandable. Fields (all additive):

| Where | Field | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `tool.result.data` | `outcome: executed \| failed \| not_executed \| unknown` | executed = ran and succeeded; failed = ran and reported failure; not_executed = refused/short-circuited before the sandbox saw it; unknown = may or may not have run (ack lost, transport, worker interrupted) |
| `tool.result.data` | `error_class` (iff `is_error`): `{origin: injected\|staged\|real, layer: boundary\|filesystem\|sandbox\|transport\|harness\|model\|gym, code, kind?, label, outcome_known, side_effect_applied?, detail?}` | provenance and layer; `label` is a fixed string rendered verbatim; `error_code` kept for compatibility; raw text stays in `output` |
| `tool.result.data` | `attempts`, `sandbox: {id, alive}` | harness-level retries of this call (1 = none); identity/liveness of the workspace that answered |
| `fault.fired.data` | `origin` (injected \| staged, never real), `layer` (boundary for injected, filesystem for staged), `description` | one plain sentence per fault |
| `episode.reset.data` | `sandbox_id` (short), `attempt` | workspace identity; >1 when re-provisioned |
| `episode.sandbox` (new) | `{sandbox_id, status: alive\|terminated\|replaced, reason, step}` | the environment noticed the worker changed |
| `interruption` (new) | `Interruption {step?, layer, code, label, tool_use_id?, tool?, path?, outcome_known, planned, resumed, worker_generation, at, detail?}` | always real; `planned: true` when a scenario `harness_faults` entry triggered it |
| `run.resumed` (new) | `{worker_generation, resumed_from_event_id, dangling_tool_use_id?, resumed_at}` | a fresh worker picked the run up |
| `run.finished.data` | `evaluation_status: ok\|failed\|skipped`, `evaluation_error?`, `steps`, `score?` | a run that could not be graded is `unevaluated`, never "ok" |
| `RunStatus` | `+ unevaluated`, `+ interrupted` | "ok" now implies a score |
| `RunRecord` | `error_class?`, `interruptions[]`, `worker_generation` | why the run ended as it did |
| `GET /scenarios` | `checks: [{id, description, weight}]`, `faults_public: [{kind, origin, layer, description}]`, `harness_faults: [{kind: worker_crash\|transport_abort, tool, path, nth, after_ms}]` | intended outcome and fault classes known before the run; the agent never sees them |
| codes (final) | `ENOENT EACCES ETIMEDOUT EINVAL ESANDBOX ETRANSPORT EHARNESS EMODEL EGYM EINTERNAL ENOEPISODE` | `ETIMEDOUT` is ack-lost *or* a real client timeout — origin/layer tell them apart |

Older records (e.g. `r_e9bc5c8c6739`) predate these fields; the UI resolves what it can from the
evaluate ledger (`outcome: ack_lost` ⇒ the write landed, `short_circuit` ⇒ nothing ran) and says
"unknown" otherwise. Web-side model: `apps/web/src/lib/reliability.ts` (`buildVerdict`), rendered by
`src/components/verdict/VerdictStrip.tsx`.

## 6. Identity

- **Minting.** `src/lib/identity.ts`: read `localStorage["faultline.user_id"]`, else the `faultline_uid`
  cookie, else mint `"u_" + crypto.randomUUID()`; write back to both. Cookie attributes:
  `SameSite=Lax; Secure; Max-Age=31536000; Path=/`. Not `HttpOnly` (JS writes it). All storage access is
  wrapped in try/catch; a browser that blocks storage still works for the session with an in-memory id.
- **Transport.** The harness API lives on a different origin from the SPA, so cookies are never sent to
  it. The id travels as `X-Faultline-User` on every request; the harness CORS config allows that header.
- **Why both stores.** localStorage is the primary (no size/attribute quirks, not sent anywhere);
  the cookie only exists so the id survives a "clear site data → local storage" that leaves cookies.
- **Threat model.** This is scoping, not authentication (explicit non-goal). Ids are 122-bit random and
  never listed, so they are unguessable in practice, but anyone holding one can read that browser's
  transcripts. Nothing sensitive is stored: scenarios are bundled, the workspace is a fixture, and the
  provider key never leaves `run_episode`. `POST …/runs` is rate-limited per id.

Vite 8 + React 19 + TypeScript 6, Tailwind v4 (`@tailwindcss/vite`), shadcn v4 CLI (style `base-nova`
on Base UI, `neutral` base colour, CSS variables, the `cn` helper package, lucide icons) for primitives,
Beautiful UI for the AI-native composites, vitest (jsdom). No client router: the URL carries
---PLAN 2.10.4---
#### 2.10.4 Reliability verdict strip (brainstorm 2026-09-12, contract in `ARCHITECTURE.md` §5.4)
Goal: a reviewer answers five questions in ten seconds without reading the transcript.
| Panel | Question | Shown as | Source (structured only) |
|---|---|---|---|
| Task | what was asked, what "done" means | scenario title + the grader's check descriptions as the intended outcome | `episode.reset.task_prompt`, scenario `checks[].description` (public) |
| Fault | what failed, where, injected or real, which layer | "step 4 · CHANGELOG.md · write applied, acknowledgement lost · injected · transport" | `fault.fired.{step,kind,path,layer,origin,description}` |
| Execution | did the command run? | executed / failed / **not executed** / **unknown** — the last two are different answers and are never merged | `tool.result.outcome`, `error.code` (dictionary), `exit_code` |
| Recovery | what the agent did next, harness retries, did the sandbox survive | "read-back before retry · 0 duplicate writes · harness attempts 1 · sandbox sb_… alive" | subsequent `tool.call`/`tool.result`, `attempts`, `sandbox.{id,alive}`, `episode.sandbox` |
| Result | diff + independent verification | "+3 −1 in 2 files · hidden tests 8/8 · recovery checks 3/3 · score 100" or "could not be graded: sandbox unavailable" | `workspace.diff`, `episode.evaluated.{tests,checks}`, `run.finished.evaluation_status` |
Every panel: a status glyph, one plain-language line, an expander with the raw event(s). Codes are explained by a dictionary, never by parsing messages.

#### 2.10.3 Layout & state
- Three columns ≥ 1280 px: rail 280 px · transcript (max-w 48rem, centred) · workspace 420 px
  (a Sheet on narrower screens).
- Transcript = ChatGPT/claude.ai shape: user bubble (task prompt) → assistant turn = StreamingText
  prose + ThinkingState trace whose steps are ToolChips (expand → CodeBlock stdout/stderr, exit code,
  duration, `fault` badge from `tool.result.fault`, `recovered` badge when the next call on the same
  path succeeds) → final summary + TaskRows score card. Header chips: status, step `n/max`, tokens, elapsed.
- Keep the existing no-router design: `?c=<conversation_id>`, `?run=<run_id>`, `?demo=<scenario_id>`.
- Keep the existing reducer (`src/lib/reducer.ts`) as the single view model for live SSE, `GET /runs/{id}`
  restore and replay; persisted transcripts (`GET /conversations/{id}` → messages/blocks) are mapped to
  the same view model so every path renders through the same components.
- `src/lib/{identity,api,config,reducer,replay,log}.ts`; vitest covers reducer, SSE resume, config, identity.

### 2.11 Failure provenance and real interruptions (added 2026-09-12 18:35 EDT)

The agent keeps seeing OS/HTTP-style errors; everything else gets provenance. Canonical types in
`schemas.py` (`ErrorClass`, `ToolOutcome`, `Interruption`, `HarnessFault`, `InterruptionReport`,
run statuses `unevaluated` / `interrupted`), contract for the UI in `docs/error-taxonomy.md`,
truthful per-injector semantics in `services/sandbox-env/FAULTS.md`.

- **origin**: `injected` (short-circuited or ack-withheld at the boundary; nothing real failed) ·
  `staged` (the scenario really deleted the file at reset; the ENOENT is a real OS error) ·
  `real` (unplanned failure of sandbox / transport / harness / model / gym / filesystem).
- **outcome** per tool result: `executed | failed | not_executed | unknown`.
- **codes**: `ENOENT EACCES ETIMEDOUT EINVAL` (agent-facing, same text either way) +
  `ESANDBOX ETRANSPORT EHARNESS EMODEL EGYM EINTERNAL ENOEPISODE` (real only).
- **scenario `worker-crash`**: the harness worker really dies (`os._exit`) while its first

/**
 * Demo replay: play a bundled RunRecord's events back with fixed spacing so the UI is fully
 * exercisable with no backend at all (a provider outage or a cold harness must not leave a reviewer
 * looking at an empty page).
 *
 * `public/demo/<scenario>.json` holds a RunRecord; scripts/export_demo.py writes those from a real
 * run under runs/. See public/demo/README.md.
 */

import { getLogger } from './log'
import type { Event, RunRecord } from './types'

const log = getLogger('web')

export const DEMO_STEP_MS = 400

export interface DemoMeta {
  id: string
  label: string
  file: string
}

/** Demos shipped in public/demo. Adding a file here is all it takes to expose a new replay. */
export const DEMOS: DemoMeta[] = [
  // A real capture: claude-haiku-4-5 on the deployed stack (run r_e9bc5c8c6739, 2026-09-12),
  // exported with scripts/export_demo.py. Its id matches the scenario id so the scenario card
  // offers it via demoFor().
  { id: 'lost-ack', label: 'lost-ack', file: '/demo/lost-ack.json' },
]

export function demoFor(scenarioId: string): DemoMeta | undefined {
  return DEMOS.find((d) => d.id === scenarioId)
}

export async function loadDemo(
  file: string,
  fetchImpl: typeof fetch = fetch,
): Promise<RunRecord> {
  const res = await fetchImpl(file, { cache: 'no-store' })
  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`demo ${file} not found (${res.status})`)
  const rec = (await res.json()) as RunRecord
  if (!Array.isArray(rec?.events)) throw new Error(`demo ${file} has no events`)
  return rec
}

export interface ReplayHandle {
  stop: () => void
}

export interface ReplayOptions {
  events: Event[]
  onEvents: (events: Event[]) => void
  onDone?: () => void
  stepMs?: number
  setTimeoutImpl?: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => unknown
  clearTimeoutImpl?: (h: unknown) => void
}

📝 Write VerdictStrip.tsx
/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web/src/components/verdict/VerdictStrip.tsx
/**
 * VerdictStrip — the ten-second reliability read at the top of a run.
 *
 * Renders a `Verdict` (src/lib/reliability.types.ts) and nothing else: five panels — task, fault,
 * execution, recovery, result — each a status glyph, one headline, one plain sentence and a few
 * fact chips. Every panel has a "details" expander that lists the structured evidence behind it
 * (incidents with their taxonomy label, the execution outcome and where that answer came from,
 * follow-up calls, grader checks) and a "raw" block with the payloads the model was built from.
 *
 * Nothing here derives an origin or an outcome from message text. A panel whose status is
 * `unknown` says so; the reason is the model's `plain` sentence. Codes are explained by the
 * dictionary in src/lib/codes.ts, never by parsing an error string.
 *
 * The expanders use `aria-expanded` / `aria-controls` on a plain button (the ThinkingTrace /
 * ScoreRows pattern) rather than the Collapsible primitive: the open details render full-width
 * under the five-column row, because a 150 px column cannot hold an incident table.
 */

import { Fragment, useId, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Box, Check, ChevronDown, CircleQuestionMark, TriangleAlert, X } from 'lucide-react'

import { CodeBlock, toolLabel } from '@/components/bui'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import { CODE_TEXT, layerText, originText } from '@/lib/codes'
import type {
  ExecutionOutcome,
  ExecutionSource,
  Fact,
  FollowUp,
  Grading,
  Incident,
  PanelKey,
  PanelStatus,
  RawRef,
  SandboxSummary,
  Verdict,
  VerdictPanel,
} from '@/lib/reliability.types'
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils'

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- vocabulary

const PANEL_ORDER: PanelKey[] = ['task', 'fault', 'execution', 'recovery', 'result']

const PANEL_LABEL: Record<PanelKey, string> = {
  task: 'Task',
  fault: 'Fault',
  execution: 'Execution',
  recovery: 'Recovery',
  result: 'Result',
}

/** The accessible name of a panel's glyph. */
const STATUS_WORD: Record<PanelStatus, string> = {
  ok: 'ok',
  warn: 'warning',
  fail: 'failed',
  unknown: 'unknown',
  pending: 'pending',
}

const STATUS_TEXT: Record<PanelStatus, string> = {
  ok: 'text-emerald-300',
  warn: 'text-amber-300',
  fail: 'text-rose-300',
  unknown: 'text-ink-3',
  pending: 'text-sky-300',
}

const CHIP_TONE: Record<PanelStatus, string> = {
  ok: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-200',
  warn: 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-200',
  fail: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-200',
  unknown: 'border-line bg-muted/30 text-ink-2',
  pending: 'border-sky-500/30 bg-sky-500/10 text-sky-200',
}

/** docs/error-taxonomy.md: injected and staged are amber, real is red. */
const ORIGIN_TONE: Record<Incident['origin'], string> = {
  injected: 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300',
  staged: 'border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300',
  real: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-300',
  unknown: 'border-line text-ink-3',
}

/** Four different answers; never merged. */
const OUTCOME_WORD: Record<ExecutionOutcome, string> = {
  executed: 'executed',
  failed: 'failed',
  not_executed: 'not executed',
  unknown: 'unknown',
}

const OUTCOME_TONE: Record<ExecutionOutcome, string> = {
  executed: 'text-emerald-300',
  failed: 'text-rose-300',
  not_executed: 'text-amber-300',
  unknown: 'text-ink-3',
}

const SOURCE_TEXT: Record<ExecutionSource, string> = {
  outcome: "from the environment's outcome field",
  error_class: 'from error class',
  ledger: 'from the ledger after grading',
  exit_code: 'exit code',
  none: 'unclassified',
}

const FOLLOW_UP_KIND: Record<FollowUp['kind'], string> = {
  read: 'read-back',
  write: 're-write',
  other: 'follow-up',
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- helpers

function originWord(origin: Incident['origin']): string {
  return origin === 'unknown' ? 'unknown' : originText(origin)
}

/** Dictionary text for a code; tolerant of the entry being a string or a record of strings. */
function codeText(code: string | null): string | null {
  if (!code) return null
  const entry: unknown = (CODE_TEXT as Record<string, unknown>)[code]
  if (typeof entry === 'string') return entry
  if (entry && typeof entry === 'object') {
    const rec = entry as Record<string, unknown>
    for (const k of ['plain', 'text', 'short', 'description', 'label']) {
      const v = rec[k]
      if (typeof v === 'string') return v
    }
  }
  return null
}

function safeJson(value: unknown): string {
  try {
    return JSON.stringify(value, null, 2) ?? 'undefined'
  } catch {
    return String(value)
  }
}

function shortId(id: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!id) return '—'
  return id.length > 14 ? `${id.slice(0, 12)}…` : id
}

/** "step 4 · Write · CHANGELOG.md" — where an incident happened, from its structured fields only. */
function where(incident: Incident): string {
  const parts: string[] = []
  if (incident.step !== null) parts.push(`step ${incident.step}`)
  if (incident.tool) parts.push(toolLabel(incident.tool))
  if (incident.path) parts.push(incident.path)
  return parts.length > 0 ? parts.join(' · ') : 'position not recorded'
}

type SandboxState = 'lost' | 'survived' | 'alive' | 'unknown'

function sandboxState(s: SandboxSummary): SandboxState {
  if (s.lost === true || s.alive === false) return 'lost'
  if (s.lost === false) return 'survived'
  if (s.alive === true) return 'alive'
  return 'unknown'
}

function sandboxStateText(s: SandboxSummary): string {
  switch (sandboxState(s)) {
    case 'lost':
      return s.lostAtStep !== null ? `lost at step ${s.lostAtStep}` : 'lost'
    case 'survived':
      return 'survived — the run was graded inside it'
    case 'alive':
      return 'alive (last report)'
    default:
      return 'unknown — the record does not say'
  }
}

function gradingBlockedText(grading: Grading, live: boolean): string {
  const base = live ? 'Not graded yet' : 'Not graded'
  return grading.blockedBy ? `${base} — ${grading.blockedBy}` : `${base}.`
}

/** Older records keep the prompt in the panel's raw payload; use it when the caller passes none. */
function rawTaskPrompt(raw: RawRef[]): string | null {
  for (const r of raw) {
    const j = r.json
    if (j && typeof j === 'object') {
      const v = (j as Record<string, unknown>).task_prompt
      if (typeof v === 'string' && v.length > 0) return v
    }
  }
  return null
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- atoms

function StatusGlyph({ status, className }: { status: PanelStatus; className?: string }) {
  const label = STATUS_WORD[status]
  if (status === 'pending') {
    return (
      <span
        role="img"
        aria-label={label}
        className={cn(
          'bui-spin inline-block size-3.5 shrink-0 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line-strong border-t-sky-300',
          className,
        )}
      />
    )
  }
  const Icon = status === 'ok' ? Check : status === 'warn' ? TriangleAlert : status === 'fail' ? X : CircleQuestionMark
  return (
    <span
      role="img"
      aria-label={label}
      className={cn('inline-flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center', STATUS_TEXT[status], className)}
    >
      <Icon className="size-3.5" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />
    </span>
  )
}

function OkGlyph({ ok }: { ok: boolean | null }) {
  if (ok === null) {
    return (
      <span role="img" aria-label="no result" className="inline-flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center text-ink-3">
        <span className="size-2 rounded-full border-[1.5px] border-line-strong" aria-hidden />
      </span>
    )
  }
  return (
    <span
      role="img"
      aria-label={ok ? 'pass' : 'fail'}
      className={cn('inline-flex size-4 shrink-0 items-center justify-center', ok ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-rose-300')}
    >
      {ok ? <Check className="size-3.5" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden /> : <X className="size-3.5" strokeWidth={2.5} aria-hidden />}
    </span>
  )
}

function FactChip({ fact }: { fact: Fact }) {
  const chip = (
    <span
      data-slot="verdict-fact"
      data-tone={fact.tone ?? 'unknown'}
      tabIndex={fact.hint ? 0 : undefined}
      className={cn(
        'inline-flex h-5 max-w-full items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-1.5 font-mono text-[10.5px] leading-none whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums',
        'focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none',
        CHIP_TONE[fact.tone ?? 'unknown'],
      )}
    >
      <span className="opacity-70">{fact.label}:</span>
      <span className="truncate">{fact.value}</span>
    </span>
  )
  if (!fact.hint) return chip
  return (
    <Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger render={chip} />
      <TooltipContent>{fact.hint}</TooltipContent>
    </Tooltip>
  )
}

function OriginBadge({ origin }: { origin: Incident['origin'] }) {
  return (
    <Badge variant="outline" data-slot="origin-badge" data-origin={origin} className={cn('font-mono', ORIGIN_TONE[origin])}>
      {originWord(origin)}
    </Badge>
  )
}

function SubHeading({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <h4 className="mb-1 text-[10.5px] font-medium tracking-wider text-ink-3 uppercase">{children}</h4>
}

function Empty({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  return <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-3">{children}</p>
}

/** A compact key/value list; `null` rows are skipped so callers can inline conditions. */
function KV({ rows }: { rows: Array<[string, ReactNode] | null> }) {
  const items = rows.filter((r): r is [string, ReactNode] => r !== null)
  if (items.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-[max-content_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 text-[12px] leading-relaxed">
      {items.map(([k, v]) => (
        <Fragment key={k}>
          <dt className="text-ink-3">{k}</dt>
          <dd className="min-w-0 break-words text-ink-2">{v}</dd>
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </dl>
  )
}

function IncidentTitle({ incident }: { incident: Incident }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1">
      <OriginBadge origin={incident.origin} />
      <span className="text-[12.5px] font-medium text-ink">{incident.label ?? 'no classification recorded'}</span>
      <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-3">{where(incident)}</span>
      {incident.planned === true ? (
        <Badge variant="outline" className="border-violet-500/30 bg-violet-500/10 font-mono text-violet-300">
          planned
        </Badge>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  )
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- details per panel

function TaskDetails({ panel, taskPrompt }: { panel: VerdictPanel; taskPrompt?: string | null }) {
  const text = taskPrompt ?? rawTaskPrompt(panel.raw)
  return (
    <div>
      <SubHeading>Task prompt</SubHeading>
      {text ? (
        <p className="text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap text-ink">{text}</p>
      ) : (
        <Empty>The task prompt was not recorded.</Empty>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

function FaultDetails({ incidents }: { incidents: Incident[] }) {
  if (incidents.length === 0) return <Empty>No incidents were recorded for this run.</Empty>
  return (
    <ol className="space-y-3">
      {incidents.map((inc) => {
        const dictionary = codeText(inc.code)
        const layer = inc.layer ? layerText(inc.layer) : null
        const detail = inc.errorClass?.detail ?? inc.interruption?.detail ?? null
        return (
          <li key={inc.id} data-slot="verdict-incident" data-origin={inc.origin} className="space-y-1.5">
            <IncidentTitle incident={inc} />
            <KV
              rows={[
                [
                  'layer',
                  inc.layer ? (
                    <>
                      <code className="font-mono">{inc.layer}</code>
                      {layer && layer !== inc.layer ? <span> — {layer}</span> : null}
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    'not recorded'
                  ),
                ],
                [
                  'code',
                  inc.code ? (
                    <>
                      <code className="font-mono">{inc.code}</code>
                      <span> — {dictionary ?? 'no dictionary entry'}</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    'not recorded'
                  ),
                ],
                inc.kind ? ['kind', <code className="font-mono">{inc.kind}</code>] : null,
                inc.path ? ['path', <code className="font-mono">{inc.path}</code>] : null,
                inc.step !== null ? ['step', String(inc.step)] : null,
                inc.planned === null
                  ? null
                  : ['planned', inc.planned ? 'yes — a scenario harness_faults entry triggered this real failure' : 'no — unplanned'],
                inc.description ? ['note', inc.description] : null,
                detail ? ['detail', <span className="font-mono text-[11.5px]">{detail}</span>] : null,
              ]}
            />
          </li>
        )
      })}
    </ol>
  )
}

function ExecutionDetails({ incidents }: { incidents: Incident[] }) {
  if (incidents.length === 0) return <Empty>No failed or faulted calls to classify.</Empty>
  return (
    <ol className="space-y-3">
      {incidents.map((inc) => (
        <li key={inc.id} data-slot="verdict-execution" data-outcome={inc.execution} className="space-y-1">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-x-2 gap-y-0.5">
            <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-3">{where(inc)}</span>
            <span className={cn('text-[12.5px] font-medium', OUTCOME_TONE[inc.execution])}>{OUTCOME_WORD[inc.execution]}</span>
            <span className="text-[11.5px] text-ink-3">{SOURCE_TEXT[inc.executionSource]}</span>
          </div>
          <ul className="space-y-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink-2">
            {inc.sideEffectApplied === true ? (
              <li>side effect applied — the write had landed</li>
            ) : inc.sideEffectApplied === false ? (
              <li>no side effect — nothing changed on disk</li>
            ) : null}
            {inc.outcomeKnown === false ? (
              <li className="text-amber-300">outcome not known to the agent at the time — verify before retrying</li>
            ) : null}
            {inc.attempts && inc.attempts > 1 ? <li>harness attempts: {inc.attempts}</li> : null}
          </ul>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ol>
  )
}

function RecoveryDetails({ verdict }: { verdict: Verdict }) {
  const { incidents, sandbox } = verdict
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      {incidents.length === 0 ? (
        <Empty>No incidents, so there was nothing to recover from.</Empty>
      ) : (
        <ol className="space-y-3">
          {incidents.map((inc) => (
            <li key={inc.id} data-slot="verdict-recovery" className="space-y-1">
              <div className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-3">{where(inc)}</div>
              {inc.followUps.length === 0 ? (
                <Empty>no later call touched this path</Empty>
              ) : (
                <ol className="space-y-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink-2">
                  {inc.followUps.map((f) => (
                    <li key={f.toolUseId} data-slot="verdict-follow-up" data-kind={f.kind} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                      <OkGlyph ok={f.ok} />
                      <span>
                        step {f.step} · {FOLLOW_UP_KIND[f.kind]} · {toolLabel(f.tool)} ·{' '}
                        {f.ok === null ? 'no result' : f.ok ? 'ok' : 'failed'}
                      </span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ol>
              )}
              <ul className="space-y-0.5 text-[12px] text-ink-2">
                {inc.attempts && inc.attempts > 1 ? <li>harness attempts: {inc.attempts}</li> : null}
                {inc.interruption ? (
                  <li>
                    {inc.interruption.resumed
                      ? `resumed by worker ${inc.interruption.worker_generation}`
                      : 'no worker resumed the run'}
                  </li>
                ) : null}
              </ul>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      )}
      <div>
        <SubHeading>Harness and sandbox</SubHeading>
        <KV
          rows={[
            ['worker generation', String(sandbox.workerGeneration)],
            ['resumes', String(sandbox.resumes)],
            ['sandbox', sandboxStateText(sandbox)],
            sandbox.sandboxId ? ['sandbox id', <code className="font-mono">{sandbox.sandboxId}</code>] : null,
            sandbox.episodeId ? ['episode', <code className="font-mono">{sandbox.episodeId}</code>] : null,
            sandbox.attempt && sandbox.attempt > 1 ? ['workspace', `re-provisioned ×${sandbox.attempt}`] : null,
          ]}
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function ResultDetails({ verdict }: { verdict: Verdict }) {
  const { diff, grading } = verdict
  const status = grading.evaluationStatus
  return (
    <div className="space-y-3">
      <div>
        <SubHeading>Workspace diff</SubHeading>
        {diff ? (
          <div className="space-y-1">
            <p className="font-mono text-[11.5px] tabular-nums">
              <span className="text-emerald-300">+{diff.added}</span> <span className="text-rose-300">−{diff.removed}</span>{' '}
              <span className="text-ink-3">
                in {diff.files} file{diff.files === 1 ? '' : 's'}
              </span>
            </p>
            {diff.paths.length > 0 ? (
              <ul className="font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-2">
                {diff.paths.map((p) => (
                  <li key={p}>{p}</li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            ) : null}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <Empty>No workspace diff was recorded.</Empty>
        )}
      </div>
      <div>
        <SubHeading>Independent checks</SubHeading>
        {status && status !== 'ok' ? (
          <p className="mb-1 text-[12px] text-amber-300" data-slot="verdict-grading-status">
            grading {status}
          </p>
        ) : null}
        {grading.available ? (
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            <p className="font-mono text-[11.5px] text-ink-2 tabular-nums">
              score {grading.score ?? '—'}
              {grading.tests
                ? ` · tests ${grading.tests.passed} passed · ${grading.tests.failed} failed · ${grading.tests.errors} errors`
                : ' · no test summary'}
            </p>
            {grading.checks.length === 0 ? (
              <Empty>The grader reported no recovery checks.</Empty>
            ) : (
              <ul className="space-y-1">
                {grading.checks.map((c) => (
                  <li key={c.id} data-slot="verdict-check" data-ok={c.ok} className="flex items-start gap-1.5 text-[12px]">
                    <OkGlyph ok={c.ok} />
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="text-ink">
                        {c.description}{' '}
                        <span className="font-mono text-[11px] text-ink-3">
                          {c.id} · weight {c.weight}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      {c.detail ? <div className="text-[11.5px] text-ink-3">{c.detail}</div> : null}
                    </div>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            )}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-[12px] text-ink-2" data-slot="verdict-grading-blocked">
            {gradingBlockedText(grading, verdict.live)}
          </p>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function RawBlocks({ raw }: { raw: RawRef[] }) {
  if (raw.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <details className="mt-2" data-slot="verdict-raw">
      <summary className="cursor-pointer text-[11px] text-ink-3 select-none hover:text-ink">
        raw ({raw.length})
      </summary>
      <div className="mt-2 space-y-2">
        {raw.map((r, i) => (
          <CodeBlock key={`${r.label}-${i}`} code={safeJson(r.json)} filename={r.label} language="json" maxHeight={260} />
        ))}
      </div>
    </details>
  )
}

function PanelDetails({ k, verdict, taskPrompt }: { k: PanelKey; verdict: Verdict; taskPrompt?: string | null }) {
  switch (k) {
    case 'task':
      return <TaskDetails panel={verdict.panels.task} taskPrompt={taskPrompt} />
    case 'fault':
      return <FaultDetails incidents={verdict.incidents} />
    case 'execution':
      return <ExecutionDetails incidents={verdict.incidents} />
    case 'recovery':
      return <RecoveryDetails verdict={verdict} />
    default:
      return <ResultDetails verdict={verdict} />
  }
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- panel

function PanelCard({
  panel,
  open,
  onToggle,
  detailsId,
}: {
  panel: VerdictPanel
  open: boolean
  onToggle: () => void
  detailsId: string
}) {
  const label = PANEL_LABEL[panel.key]
  return (
    <article
      data-slot="verdict-panel"
      data-key={panel.key}
      data-status={panel.status}
      aria-label={`${label} panel`}
      className="flex min-w-0 flex-col gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-3"
    >
      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
        <StatusGlyph status={panel.status} />
        <span className="text-[10px] font-medium tracking-wider text-ink-3 uppercase">{label}</span>
        <button
          type="button"
          aria-expanded={open}
          aria-controls={detailsId}
          aria-label={`${label} details`}
          onClick={onToggle}
          className={cn(
            '-mr-1 ml-auto flex h-5 items-center gap-0.5 rounded-md px-1 text-[11px] text-ink-3 transition-colors duration-100',
            'hover:bg-muted/40 hover:text-ink focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none',
            open && 'text-ink',
          )}
        >
          Details
          <ChevronDown className={cn('size-3 transition-transform duration-200', open && 'rotate-180')} aria-hidden />
        </button>
      </div>
      <p className="line-clamp-3 text-[13px] leading-snug font-medium text-ink" title={panel.headline}>
        {panel.headline}
      </p>
      <p className="line-clamp-2 text-[11.5px] leading-snug text-ink-3" title={panel.plain}>
        {panel.plain}
      </p>
      {panel.facts.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="mt-auto flex flex-wrap gap-1 pt-0.5">
          {panel.facts.map((f, i) => (
            <FactChip key={`${f.label}-${i}`} fact={f} />
          ))}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </article>
  )
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- strip

export interface VerdictStripProps {
  verdict: Verdict | null | undefined
  /** The full task prompt for the task panel's details (the model keeps only a headline). */
  taskPrompt?: string | null
  className?: string
}

export function VerdictStrip({ verdict, taskPrompt, className }: VerdictStripProps) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState<Partial<Record<PanelKey, boolean>>>({})
  const baseId = useId()
  if (!verdict) return null

  const toggle = (k: PanelKey) => setOpen((cur) => ({ ...cur, [k]: !cur[k] }))
  const openKeys = PANEL_ORDER.filter((k) => open[k])

  return (
    <section
      aria-label="Run verdict"
      data-slot="verdict-strip"
      data-status={verdict.status ?? 'unknown'}
      data-live={verdict.live}
      className={cn('overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-line bg-surface', className)}
    >
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 divide-y divide-line lg:grid-cols-5 lg:divide-x lg:divide-y-0">
        {PANEL_ORDER.map((k) => (
          <PanelCard
            key={k}
            panel={verdict.panels[k]}
            open={!!open[k]}
            onToggle={() => toggle(k)}
            detailsId={`${baseId}-${k}`}
          />
        ))}
      </div>
      {openKeys.map((k) => (
        <div
          key={k}
          id={`${baseId}-${k}`}
          role="region"
          aria-label={`${PANEL_LABEL[k]} details`}
          data-slot="verdict-details"
          data-key={k}
          className="bui-fade-in border-t border-line px-3.5 py-3"
        >
          <div className="mb-2 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[10px] font-medium tracking-wider text-ink-3 uppercase">
            <StatusGlyph status={verdict.panels[k].status} />
            {PANEL_LABEL[k]} · details
          </div>
          <PanelDetails k={k} verdict={verdict} taskPrompt={taskPrompt} />
          <RawBlocks raw={verdict.panels[k].raw} />
        </div>
      ))}
    </section>
  )
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------- sandbox chip

export interface SandboxChipProps {
  sandbox: SandboxSummary | null | undefined
  className?: string
}

const SANDBOX_TONE: Record<SandboxState, string> = {
  lost: 'border-rose-500/30 bg-rose-500/10 text-rose-300',
  survived: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300',
  alive: 'border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-300',
  unknown: 'border-border text-muted-foreground',
}

/** Top-bar chip: which workspace answered, which worker drove it, and whether it survived. */
export function SandboxChip({ sandbox, className }: SandboxChipProps) {
  if (!sandbox) return null
  const id = sandbox.sandboxId ?? sandbox.episodeId
  const state = sandboxState(sandbox)
  const reprovisioned = !!sandbox.attempt && sandbox.attempt > 1
  if (!id && state === 'unknown' && sandbox.resumes === 0 && !reprovisioned) return null

  const text = `${shortId(id)} · worker ${sandbox.workerGeneration} · ${state}${reprovisioned ? ` · re-provisioned ×${sandbox.attempt}` : ''}`
  return (
    <Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger
        render={
          <Badge
            variant="outline"
            data-slot="sandbox-chip"
            data-sandbox={state}
            className={cn('font-mono', SANDBOX_TONE[state], className)}
          >
            <Box aria-hidden /> {text}
          </Badge>
        }
      />
      <TooltipContent className="flex-col items-start gap-0.5">
        <span>sandbox {sandbox.sandboxId ?? '—'}</span>
        <span>episode {sandbox.episodeId ?? '—'}</span>
        <span>
          worker generation {sandbox.workerGeneration} · {sandbox.resumes} resume{sandbox.resumes === 1 ? '' : 's'}
        </span>
        <span>{sandboxStateText(sandbox)}</span>
      </TooltipContent>
    </Tooltip>
  )
}

📝 Write VerdictStrip.test.tsx
/Users/sohamwaychal/Documents/anthropic/apps/web/src/components/verdict/VerdictStrip.test.tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'

import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'

import { SandboxChip, VerdictStrip } from './VerdictStrip'
import { ackLostRecovered, allUnknown, sandboxLost } from './test-fixtures'

function renderStrip(ui: React.ReactElement) {
  return render(<TooltipProvider>{ui}</TooltipProvider>)
}

function panel(key: string): HTMLElement {
  return document.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-slot="verdict-panel"][data-key="${key}"]`)!
}

function openDetails(key: string): HTMLElement {
  const label = key.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + key.slice(1)
  fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: `${label} details` }))
  return screen.getByRole('region', { name: `${label} details` })
}

function factChips(key: string): string[] {
  return Array.from(panel(key).querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-fact"]')).map((el) => el.textContent ?? '')
}

describe('VerdictStrip', () => {
  it('renders nothing without a verdict', () => {
    const { container } = renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={null} />)
    expect(container.querySelector('[data-slot="verdict-strip"]')).toBeNull()
  })

  it('reads an injected ack_lost run that recovered and scored 100', () => {
    renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={ackLostRecovered} taskPrompt="Release 0.2.0 …" />)

    const strip = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Run verdict' })
    expect(strip.getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('ok')
    expect(within(strip).getAllByRole('article')).toHaveLength(5)

    // the five headlines and their status glyphs
    expect(screen.getByText('Release 0.2.0: changelog entry and version bump')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Simulated lost ack on CHANGELOG.md at step 2')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('The write executed — the ledger confirms it landed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Read back before writing again; sandbox survived')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Score 100 · tests 8/8 · checks 3/3')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(panel('task')).getByRole('img', { name: 'ok' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(panel('fault')).getByRole('img', { name: 'warning' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(panel('fault').getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('warn')
    expect(within(panel('result')).getByRole('img', { name: 'ok' })).toBeTruthy()

    // fact chips render "label: value" with the model's tone
    expect(factChips('fault')).toEqual(['origin:simulated', 'layer:boundary', 'code:ETIMEDOUT'])
    const originChip = panel('fault').querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-fact"]')!
    expect(originChip.getAttribute('data-tone')).toBe('warn')
    expect(factChips('result')).toEqual(['score:100', 'tests:8/8', 'checks:3/3'])

    // fault details: origin badge + verbatim taxonomy label + dictionary text for the code
    const fault = openDetails('fault')
    const badge = fault.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="origin-badge"]')!
    expect(badge.textContent).toBe('simulated')
    expect(badge.getAttribute('data-origin')).toBe('injected')
    expect(within(fault).getByText('simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('step 2 · Write · CHANGELOG.md')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('ETIMEDOUT')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('The write was applied and the acknowledgement withheld.')).toBeTruthy()

    // execution details: "executed", sourced from the ledger, the write had landed, verify hint
    const execution = openDetails('execution')
    const row = execution.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-execution"]')!
    expect(row.getAttribute('data-outcome')).toBe('executed')
    expect(within(row).getByText('executed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('from the ledger after grading')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('side effect applied — the write had landed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText(/verify before retrying/)).toBeTruthy()

    // recovery details: the read-back follow-up and the sandbox summary
    const recovery = openDetails('recovery')
    const followUp = recovery.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-follow-up"]')!
    expect(followUp.getAttribute('data-kind')).toBe('read')
    expect(within(followUp).getByRole('img', { name: 'pass' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(followUp).getByText('step 3 · read-back · Read · ok')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('survived — the run was graded inside it')).toBeTruthy()

    // result details: diff paths, tests, every check with its description
    const result = openDetails('result')
    expect(within(result).getByText('+7')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('−0')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('src/ratelimiter/version.py')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText(/tests 8 passed · 0 failed · 0 errors/)).toBeTruthy()
    const checks = result.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-check"]')
    expect(checks).toHaveLength(3)
    expect(within(checks[0]!).getByRole('img', { name: 'pass' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(checks[0]!).getByText(/read CHANGELOG.md before writing it again/)).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(checks[0]!).getByText('read_file CHANGELOG.md at step 3')).toBeTruthy()

    // task details: the full prompt passed in
    const task = openDetails('task')
    expect(within(task).getByText('Release 0.2.0 …')).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it('reads a real sandbox loss as a failure with grading blocked', () => {
    renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={sandboxLost} />)

    expect(screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Run verdict' }).getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('interrupted')
    expect(panel('fault').getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('fail')
    expect(within(panel('fault')).getByRole('img', { name: 'failed' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(panel('result')).getByRole('img', { name: 'failed' })).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Real sandbox loss at step 5')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(screen.getByText('Not graded — sandbox unavailable')).toBeTruthy()

    const fault = openDetails('fault')
    const badge = fault.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="origin-badge"]')!
    expect(badge.textContent).toBe('real')
    expect(badge.getAttribute('data-origin')).toBe('real')
    expect(within(fault).getByText('real: sandbox terminated or unavailable')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('no — unplanned')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getByText('ESANDBOX')).toBeTruthy()

    const execution = openDetails('execution')
    const row = execution.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-execution"]')!
    expect(row.getAttribute('data-outcome')).toBe('not_executed')
    expect(within(row).getByText('not executed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('from error class')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('no side effect — nothing changed on disk')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('harness attempts: 3')).toBeTruthy()

    const recovery = openDetails('recovery')
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('no later call touched this path')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('no worker resumed the run')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('lost at step 5')).toBeTruthy()

    const result = openDetails('result')
    expect(within(result).getByText('No workspace diff was recorded.')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('grading skipped')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(result).getByText('Not graded — real: sandbox terminated or unavailable')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(result.querySelectorAll('[data-slot="verdict-check"]')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('says "unknown" for a pre-taxonomy record and never fabricates an outcome', () => {
    renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={allUnknown} />)

    for (const key of ['fault', 'execution', 'recovery', 'result']) {
      expect(panel(key).getAttribute('data-status')).toBe('unknown')
      expect(within(panel(key)).getByRole('img', { name: 'unknown' })).toBeTruthy()
    }
    expect(screen.getByText('Unknown whether the call executed')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(factChips('execution')).toEqual(['outcome:unknown'])

    const fault = openDetails('fault')
    const badge = fault.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="origin-badge"]')!
    expect(badge.textContent).toBe('unknown')
    expect(badge.getAttribute('data-origin')).toBe('unknown')
    expect(within(fault).getByText('no classification recorded')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(fault).getAllByText('not recorded')).toHaveLength(2) // layer, code

    const execution = openDetails('execution')
    const row = execution.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-execution"]')!
    expect(row.getAttribute('data-outcome')).toBe('unknown')
    expect(within(row).getByText('unknown')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).getByText('unclassified')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(row).queryByText('executed')).toBeNull()
    expect(within(row).queryByText('not executed')).toBeNull()
    expect(within(row).queryByText('failed')).toBeNull()
    expect(within(row).queryByText(/the write had landed/)).toBeNull()

    const recovery = openDetails('recovery')
    expect(within(recovery).getByText('unknown — the record does not say')).toBeTruthy()

    const result = openDetails('result')
    expect(within(result).getByText('Not graded.')).toBeTruthy()
  })

  it('toggles details with aria-expanded and reveals the raw JSON', () => {
    renderStrip(<VerdictStrip verdict={ackLostRecovered} />)
    const toggle = screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Fault details' })
    expect(toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false')
    expect(screen.queryByRole('region', { name: 'Fault details' })).toBeNull()
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-slot="verdict-raw"]')).toBeNull()

    fireEvent.click(toggle)
    expect(toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('true')
    const region = screen.getByRole('region', { name: 'Fault details' })
    expect(region.id).toBe(toggle.getAttribute('aria-controls'))

    const raw = region.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="verdict-raw"]')!
    expect(raw).not.toBeNull()
    expect(within(raw).getByText('raw (1)')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(within(raw).getByText('fault.fired')).toBeTruthy()
    expect(raw.textContent).toContain('"ack_lost"')
    expect(raw.textContent).toContain('"injected"')
    expect(raw.querySelectorAll('[data-line-kind="code"]').length).toBeGreaterThan(3)

    fireEvent.click(toggle)
    expect(toggle.getAttribute('aria-expanded')).toBe('false')
    expect(screen.queryByRole('region', { name: 'Fault details' })).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('SandboxChip', () => {
  it('shows the sandbox id, worker and survival', () => {
    renderStrip(<SandboxChip sandbox={ackLostRecovered.sandbox} />)
    const chip = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="sandbox-chip"]')!
    expect(chip.textContent).toContain('sb_1a2b3c · worker 1 · survived')
    expect(chip.getAttribute('data-sandbox')).toBe('survived')
  })

  it('turns red when the sandbox was lost and notes re-provisioning', () => {
    renderStrip(<SandboxChip sandbox={{ ...sandboxLost.sandbox, attempt: 2 }} />)
    const chip = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="sandbox-chip"]')!
    expect(chip.textContent).toContain('sb_9f8e7d · worker 1 · lost · re-provisioned ×2')
    expect(chip.getAttribute('data-sandbox')).toBe('lost')
    expect(chip.className).toContain('rose')
  })

  it('falls back to the episode id and says unknown when nothing was observed', () => {
    renderStrip(<SandboxChip sandbox={allUnknown.sandbox} />)
    const chip = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-slot="sandbox-chip"]')!
    expect(chip.textContent).toContain('ep_0ld000000… · worker 1 · unknown')
    expect(chip.getAttribute('data-sandbox')).toBe('unknown')
  })

  it('renders nothing before an episode exists', () => {
    renderStrip(<SandboxChip sandbox={{ episodeId: null, workerGeneration: 1, resumes: 0, lost: null, lostAtStep: null }} />)
    expect(document.querySelector('[data-slot="sandbox-chip"]')).toBeNull()
  })
})

Tool reply2026-09-12T22:45:35.251Z
/**
 * Plain language for the error taxonomy (docs/error-taxonomy.md, packages/common schemas.py).
 *
 * These are the ONLY static mappings the reliability model is allowed to apply: code → what it
 * means, (origin, kind | layer) → the fixed label the taxonomy prescribes, origin/layer → a
 * reviewer-friendly word, exit code → its conventional meaning. None of them look at message text,
 * and none of them can tell injected from real — only `origin` (a structured field) can.
 */

import type { ErrorCode, ErrorLayer, ErrorOrigin, FaultKind } from './types'
import { ERROR_CODES } from './types'

export interface CodeText {
  /** A few words, e.g. "no such file". */
  title: string
  /** One or two sentences a reviewer can act on. */
  plain: string
  /** What to do before retrying, when the outcome may be unknown. */
  verifyHint?: string
}

export const CODE_TEXT: Record<ErrorCode, CodeText> = {
  ENOENT: {
    title: 'no such file or directory',
    plain:
      'The path could not be found. Either the file is really absent (deleted at reset, or never created) or the environment withheld it for a few calls while it stayed on disk.',
  },
  EACCES: {
    title: 'permission denied',
    plain:
      'The write was refused and nothing was written. A later write to the same path can still succeed once the refusal lifts.',
  },
  ETIMEDOUT: {
    title: 'response never arrived',
    plain:
      'The response never arrived. The operation may or may not have completed — read the file back before retrying.',
    verifyHint: 'Read the file back before writing it again; a blind retry can apply the change twice.',
  },
  EINVAL: {
    title: 'invalid argument',
    plain:
      'The call was rejected before anything ran: a path outside /workspace, a bad mode, or a directory where a file was expected.',
  },
  ESANDBOX: {
    title: 'sandbox unavailable',
    plain:
      'The sandbox that holds the workspace is gone or unreachable (terminated, not found, or its exec failed). Nothing ran, and later calls will fail the same way until a new sandbox exists.',
  },
  ETRANSPORT: {
    title: 'transport failure',
    plain:
      'The HTTP/MCP hop between the harness and sandbox-env failed (connection reset, 5xx, or the client aborted). The server may still have completed the call.',
    verifyHint: 'Verify the workspace before retrying; the request may have reached the server.',
  },
  EHARNESS: {
    title: 'worker interrupted',
    plain:
      'The harness worker itself was interrupted while this call was in flight. The request had already been dispatched, so the side effect may have landed; the ledger has the truth after the fact.',
    verifyHint: 'Read the file back before writing it again; the interrupted write may have landed.',
  },
  EMODEL: {
    title: 'model API error',
    plain: 'The model API call that drives the turn failed. No tool ran; the harness retries or ends the run.',
  },
  EGYM: {
    title: 'gym control-plane failure',
    plain:
      'A gym control-plane operation (reset, observe, evaluate or delete) failed. This is the environment, not the agent: a run that finished its work can still be left ungraded.',
  },
  EINTERNAL: {
    title: 'internal error in sandbox-env',
    plain: 'An unexpected exception inside sandbox-env (a bug; see detail). The call did not reach the sandbox.',
  },
  ENOEPISODE: {
    title: 'unknown episode',
    plain: 'The request carried a missing or unknown episode id, so sandbox-env refused it before anything ran.',
  },
}

/** The dictionary entry for a code, or null for an unknown/absent code. */
export function codeText(code: string | null | undefined): CodeText | null {
  if (!code) return null
  return (ERROR_CODES as readonly string[]).includes(code) ? CODE_TEXT[code as ErrorCode] : null
}

/** Conventional meaning of a shell exit status. */
export function exitCodeText(n: number | null | undefined): string {
  if (n === null || n === undefined || !Number.isFinite(n)) return 'no exit code'
  switch (n) {
    case 0:
      return 'exit 0 (success)'
    case 1:
      return 'exit 1 (general error)'
    case 2:
      return 'exit 2 (usage error)'
    case 124:
      return 'exit 124 (timed out)'
    case 126:
      return 'exit 126 (not executable)'
    case 127:
      return 'exit 127 (command not found)'
    case 130:
      return 'exit 130 (interrupted, SIGINT)'
    case 137:
      return 'exit 137 (killed, SIGKILL)'
    case 143:
      return 'exit 143 (terminated, SIGTERM)'
    default:
      return n > 128 ? `exit ${n} (killed by signal ${n - 128})` : `exit ${n} (failed)`
  }
}

/** Badge word for an origin: injected → "simulated", staged → "staged", real → "real". */
export function originText(origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown' | null | undefined): string {
  switch (origin) {
    case 'injected':
      return 'simulated'
    case 'staged':
      return 'staged'
    case 'real':
      return 'real'
    default:
      return 'unknown'
  }
}

/** One phrase per origin explaining what it means for the run. */
export function originPlain(origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown' | null | undefined): string {
  switch (origin) {
    case 'injected':
      return 'intercepted at the tool boundary by the scenario; nothing real failed'
    case 'staged':
      return 'the scenario changed the workspace at reset, so this is a genuine OS error on purpose'
    case 'real':
      return 'an unplanned failure of a real component'
    default:
      return 'the record does not say who caused it'
  }
}

export function layerText(layer: ErrorLayer | null | undefined): string {
  switch (layer) {
    case 'boundary':
      return 'tool boundary (sandbox-env)'
    case 'filesystem':
      return 'sandbox filesystem'
    case 'sandbox':
      return 'sandbox (Modal)'
    case 'transport':
      return 'transport (harness ↔ sandbox-env)'
    case 'harness':
      return 'harness worker'
    case 'model':
      return 'model API'
    case 'gym':
      return 'gym control plane'
    default:
      return 'unknown layer'
  }
}

/** Fixed labels the taxonomy prescribes for injected/staged faults, by kind. */
const INJECTED_LABEL: Record<FaultKind, string> = {
  missing_file: 'simulated: missing file (file still on disk)',
  denied_write: 'simulated: write denied (nothing written)',
  ack_lost: 'simulated: lost ack (write landed; response withheld)',
}

const STAGED_LABEL: Partial<Record<FaultKind, string>> = {
  missing_file: 'staged: file absent since reset',
}

/** Fixed labels the taxonomy prescribes for real failures, by layer. */
const REAL_LABEL: Record<ErrorLayer, string> = {
  filesystem: 'real: OS error in sandbox',
  sandbox: 'real: sandbox terminated or unavailable',
  transport: 'real: transport failure harness<->sandbox-env (outcome unknown)',
  harness: 'real: harness worker interrupted mid-call (outcome unknown)',
  model: 'real: model API error',
  gym: 'real: gym control-plane failure',
  boundary: 'real: internal error in sandbox-env',
}

/**
 * The taxonomy's own (origin, kind) / (origin, layer) → label table, for records that carry the
 * provenance fields but no `label` (e.g. a `fault.fired` without an `error_class`). Returns null
 * when the table has no row — never guesses.
 */
export function taxonomyLabel(
  origin: ErrorOrigin | 'unknown' | null | undefined,
  kind: FaultKind | string | null | undefined,
  layer: ErrorLayer | null | undefined,
): string | null {
  if (origin === 'injected') return (kind && INJECTED_LABEL[kind as FaultKind]) ?? null
  if (origin === 'staged') return (kind && STAGED_LABEL[kind as FaultKind]) ?? null
  if (origin === 'real') return (layer && REAL_LABEL[layer]) ?? null
  return null
}

/** The code the taxonomy pairs with an injected fault kind (missing_file → ENOENT, …). */
export function codeForInjectedKind(kind: FaultKind | string | null | undefined): ErrorCode | null {
  switch (kind) {
    case 'missing_file':
      return 'ENOENT'
    case 'denied_write':
      return 'EACCES'
    case 'ack_lost':
      return 'ETIMEDOUT'
    default:
      return null
  }
}

/** A compact key/value list; `null` rows are skipped so callers can inline conditions. */
function KV({ rows }: { rows: Array<[string, ReactNode] | null> }) {
  const items = rows.filter((r): r is [string, ReactNode] => r !== null)
  if (items.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-[max-content_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 text-[12px] leading-relaxed">
      {items.map(([k, v]) => (
        <Fragment key={k}>
          <dt className="text-ink-3">{k}</dt>
          <dd className="min-w-0 break-words text-ink-2">{v}</dd>
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </dl>
  )
}
+
interface KVRow {
  k: string
  v: ReactNode
}

/** A compact key/value list; `null` rows are skipped so callers can inline conditions. */
function KV({ rows }: { rows: Array<KVRow | null> }) {
  const items = rows.filter((r): r is KVRow => r !== null)
  if (items.length === 0) return null
  return (
    <dl className="grid grid-cols-[max-content_minmax(0,1fr)] gap-x-3 gap-y-0.5 text-[12px] leading-relaxed">
      {items.map(({ k, v }) => (
        <Fragment key={k}>
          <dt className="text-ink-3">{k}</dt>
          <dd className="min-w-0 break-words text-ink-2">{v}</dd>
        </Fragment>
      ))}
    </dl>
  )
}

function originWord(origin: Incident['origin']): string {
  return origin === 'unknown' ? 'unknown' : originText(origin)
}

/** Dictionary text for a code; tolerant of the entry being a string or a record of strings. */
function codeText(code: string | null): string | null {
  if (!code) return null
  const entry: unknown = (CODE_TEXT as Record<string, unknown>)[code]
  if (typeof entry === 'string') return entry
  if (entry && typeof entry === 'object') {
    const rec = entry as Record<string, unknown>
    for (const k of ['plain', 'text', 'short', 'description', 'label']) {
      const v = rec[k]
      if (typeof v === 'string') return v
    }
  }
  return null
}

function safeJson(value: unknown): string {
+

+
function FactChip({ fact }: { fact: Fact }) {
  const full = `${fact.label}: ${fact.value}`
  // A long value truncates in a narrow cell; the full text lives in the tooltip (or the native
  // title when the fact has no hint) and in the details region.
  const chip = (
    <span
      data-slot="verdict-fact"
      data-tone={fact.tone ?? 'unknown'}
      tabIndex={fact.hint ? 0 : undefined}
      title={fact.hint ? undefined : full}
      className={cn(
        'inline-flex h-5 max-w-full min-w-0 items-center gap-1 rounded-md border px-1.5 font-mono text-[10.5px] leading-none whitespace-nowrap tabular-nums',
        'focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring/50 focus-visible:outline-none',
        CHIP_TONE[fact.tone ?? 'unknown'],
      )}
    >
      <span className="shrink-0 opacity-70">{fact.label}:</span>
      <span className="min-w-0 truncate">{fact.value}</span>
    </span>
  )
  if (!fact.hint) return chip
  return (
    <Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger render={chip} />
      <TooltipContent className="flex-col items-start gap-0.5">
        <span className="font-mono">{full}</span>
        <span>{fact.hint}</span>
      </TooltipContent>
    </Tooltip>
  )
}

Report

What was built

New: src/components/verdict/ (VerdictStrip.tsx, index.ts, test-fixtures.ts, VerdictStrip.test.tsx)

  • VerdictStrip({ verdict: Verdict | null | undefined, taskPrompt?: string | null, className?: string }) — <section aria-label="Run verdict"> holding five <article> panels (task / fault / execution / recovery / result). Each shows a role="img" glyph (✓ emerald, ⚠ amber, ✕ rose, ? muted, animated ring for pending; aria-label = ok / warning / failed / unknown / pending), the uppercase label, headline, plain, and facts as label: value chips toned by fact.tone with a tooltip carrying hint. A chevron button per panel (aria-expanded, aria-controls, name "<Label> details") opens a full-width region under the grid: fault → incident list (origin badge via originText, verbatim label, layer via layerText, code + codeText().title/plain, path, step, planned marker, environment description, detail); execution → outcome word + source text (incl. the new 'outcome' → "from the environment's outcome field"), side_effect_applied ("the write had landed" / "nothing changed on disk"), "verify before retrying" (+ verifyHint) when outcomeKnown === false, "harness attempts: n" when > 1; recovery → follow-ups (read-back / re-write / ok / failed), harness attempts, interruption resumed-by-worker, worker generation/resumes, sandbox survived/lost-at-step/unknown, "re-provisioned ×n"; result → diff +a −r in n files + paths, tests, every check with description/✓✕/detail, "grading skipped|failed" from evaluationStatus, "Not graded — "; task → the full prompt (prop, falling back to raw[].json.task_prompt). Every region ends in a <details> "raw (n)" block rendering raw[].json through CodeBlock language="json".
  • SandboxChip({ sandbox: SandboxSummary | null | undefined, className? }) — sandboxId ?? episodeId (shortened) · worker n · lost / survived / alive / unknown (alive === false ⇒ lost, rose), plus re-provisioned ×attempt; returns null before any episode exists.
  • The expanders are a hand-rolled aria-expanded/aria-controls button (the ThinkingTrace/ScoreRows pattern) rather than the Base UI Collapsible: the open details must render full-width below the five-column row, which a per-cell Collapsible cannot do. The grid is sized by container queries (@container, 1 → @md 2-up with the result panel spanning → @2xl five across) because the transcript column is 340–720 px regardless of viewport — a viewport lg: breakpoint produced 65 px cells in the real app (screenshot-verified before and after).

Edits (within the allowed set only): RunStatusStrip.tsx (unevaluated violet / interrupted rose tones; sandbox?: SandboxSummary | null prop → <SandboxChip>), app-sidebar.tsx (STATUS_DOT for both statuses), TranscriptView.tsx (verdict?: Verdict | null, renders the strip above the task bubble, hidden only while loading with no turns), and ConversationPage / RunPage / ReplayPage (useMemo(() => buildVerdict(rv.state, scenario, { live })); Run/Replay look the scenario up from harness.scenarios by rv.state.scenarioId; Conversation passes null when no run is selected).

Fixtures (test-fixtures.ts)

  1. ackLostRecovered — status ok, injected/boundary ETIMEDOUT ack_lost incident, execution: 'executed' from 'ledger', sideEffectApplied: true, outcomeKnown: false, one read follow-up, sandbox sb_1a2b3c alive/survived, grading 100 with three checks, diff +7/−0 in 2 files.
  2. sandboxLost — status interrupted, real/sandbox ESANDBOX incident with an Interruption (planned: false, resumed: false), not_executed from error_class, attempts: 3, sandbox alive: false/lost: true/lostAtStep: 5, grading unavailable, evaluationStatus: 'skipped', blockedBy set, diff: null.
  3. allUnknown — status error, incident with origin unknown, null layer/code/label, execution: 'unknown' from 'none', all nulls; four panels unknown.
